--- ARQUIVO: src\app\actions\calendar.ts ---
"use server";

import {
  AppointmentStatus,
  Prisma,
  TransactionStatus,
  TransactionType,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import {
  getDateInTimezone,
  getWeekdayInTimezone,
  isAvailableWorkingHoursSlot,
  parseDateTimeInTimezone,
} from "@/lib/schedule";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";

const activeAppointmentStatuses: AppointmentStatus[] = [
  AppointmentStatus.PENDING,
  AppointmentStatus.CONFIRMED,
];

class SlotValidationError extends Error {}

function readDateTime(value: FormDataEntryValue | null, timeZone: string) {
  return typeof value === "string"
    ? parseDateTimeInTimezone(value, timeZone)
    : null;
}

function getMinuteOfDay(date: Date, timeZone: string): number {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone,
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    hourCycle: "h23",
  }).formatToParts(date);
  const values = Object.fromEntries(
    parts
      .filter((part) => part.type !== "literal")
      .map((part) => [part.type, Number(part.value)]),
  );

  return values.hour * 60 + values.minute;
}

async function ensureSlotIsAvailable(
  transaction: Prisma.TransactionClient,
  professionalId: string,
  timeZone: string,
  startsAt: Date,
  endsAt: Date,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const weekday = getWeekdayInTimezone(startsAt, timeZone);
  const localStartMinute = getMinuteOfDay(startsAt, timeZone);
  const localEndMinute = getMinuteOfDay(endsAt, timeZone);
  const startDate = getDateInTimezone(startsAt, timeZone);
  const endDate = getDateInTimezone(endsAt, timeZone);

  if (startDate !== endDate) {
    return "O atendimento deve terminar no mesmo dia em que começa.";
  }

  const workingHours = await transaction.workingHours.findUnique({
    where: { professionalId_weekday: { professionalId, weekday } },
  });

  if (
    !workingHours?.isActive ||
    !isAvailableWorkingHoursSlot(
      localStartMinute,
      localEndMinute,
      workingHours,
    )
  ) {
    return "O horário está fora do expediente ou coincide com uma pausa configurada.";
  }

  const [appointmentConflict, blockConflict] = await Promise.all([
    transaction.appointment.findFirst({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        status: { in: activeAppointmentStatuses },
        startsAt: { lt: endsAt },
        endsAt: { gt: startsAt },
      },
      select: { id: true },
    }),
    transaction.calendarBlock.findFirst({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        startsAt: { lt: endsAt },
        endsAt: { gt: startsAt },
      },
      select: { id: true },
    }),
  ]);

  if (appointmentConflict || blockConflict) {
    return "Este horário conflita com outro atendimento ou bloqueio.";
  }

  return null;
}

export async function createAppointmentAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const clientId = formData.get("clientId");
  const serviceId = formData.get("serviceId");

  if (
    typeof clientId !== "string" ||
    !clientId.trim() ||
    typeof serviceId !== "string" ||
    !serviceId.trim()
  ) {
    return { error: "Selecione um cliente e um serviço." };
  }

  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUnique({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { timezone: true },
  });

  if (!professional) {
    return { error: "Profissional não encontrado." };
  }

  const startsAt = readDateTime(formData.get("startsAt"), professional.timezone);

  if (!startsAt || startsAt <= new Date()) {
    return { error: "Escolha uma data e horário futuros válidos." };
  }

  const [client, service] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.client.findFirst({
      where: { id: clientId, professionalId },
      select: { id: true, name: true },
    }),
    prisma.service.findFirst({
      where: { id: serviceId, professionalId, isActive: true },
      select: { id: true, name: true, price: true, durationMinutes: true },
    }),
  ]);

  if (!client) {
    return { error: "Cliente não encontrado nesta conta." };
  }

  if (!service) {
    return { error: "O serviço selecionado não está ativo nesta conta." };
  }

  const endsAt = new Date(
    startsAt.getTime() + service.durationMinutes * 60_000,
  );
  const rawNotes = formData.get("notes");

  if (rawNotes !== null && typeof rawNotes !== "string") {
    return { error: "Observações inválidas." };
  }

  const notes = typeof rawNotes === "string" ? rawNotes.trim() || null : null;

  if ((notes?.length ?? 0) > 1000) {
    return { error: "As observações devem ter no máximo 1.000 caracteres." };
  }

  try {
    await prisma.$transaction(
      async (transaction) => {
        const conflict = await ensureSlotIsAvailable(
          transaction,
          professionalId,
          professional.timezone,
          startsAt,
          endsAt,
        );

        if (conflict) {
          throw new SlotValidationError(conflict);
        }

        const appointment = await transaction.appointment.create({
          data: {
            professionalId,
            clientId: client.id,
            serviceId: service.id,
            startsAt,
            endsAt,
            status: AppointmentStatus.CONFIRMED,
            priceAtBooking: service.price,
            notes,
          },
          select: { id: true },
        });

        await transaction.transaction.create({
          data: {
            professionalId,
            appointmentId: appointment.id,
            type: "INCOME",
            status: "PENDING",
            amount: service.price,
            description: `${service.name} · ${client.name}`,
            category: "Atendimento",
            dueAt: startsAt,
          },
        });
      },
      { isolationLevel: Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof SlotValidationError) {
      return { error: error.message };
    }

    if (
      error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2034"
    ) {
      return {
        error:
          "O horário foi reservado por outra operação. Atualize a agenda e tente novamente.",
      };
    }

    throw error;
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/clients");
  return { success: "Atendimento agendado." };
}

export async function deleteCalendarBlockAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const blockId = formData.get("blockId");

  if (typeof blockId !== "string" || !blockId.trim()) {
    return { error: "Bloqueio inválido." };
  }

  const result = await getPrismaClient().calendarBlock.deleteMany({
    where: { id: blockId, professionalId },
  });

  if (result.count !== 1) {
    return { error: "Bloqueio não encontrado nesta conta." };
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  return { success: "Bloqueio removido." };
}

export async function updateAppointmentStatusAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const appointmentId = formData.get("appointmentId");
  const intent = formData.get("intent");

  if (
    typeof appointmentId !== "string" ||
    !appointmentId.trim() ||
    (intent !== "cancel" && intent !== "complete" && intent !== "confirm")
  ) {
    return { error: "Ação de atendimento inválida." };
  }

  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const appointment = await prisma.appointment.findFirst({
    where: { id: appointmentId, professionalId },
    select: {
      id: true,
      startsAt: true,
      status: true,
      priceAtBooking: true,
      service: { select: { name: true, materialCost: true } },
      client: { select: { name: true } },
    },
  });

  if (!appointment) {
    return { error: "Atendimento não encontrado nesta conta." };
  }

  if (
    intent === "confirm" &&
    appointment.status !== AppointmentStatus.PENDING
  ) {
    return { error: "Somente solicitações pendentes podem ser confirmadas." };
  }

  if (!activeAppointmentStatuses.includes(appointment.status)) {
    return { error: "Este atendimento já foi encerrado." };
  }

  if (intent === "complete" && appointment.startsAt > new Date()) {
    return { error: "Não é possível concluir um atendimento futuro." };
  }

  const nextStatus =
    intent === "cancel"
      ? AppointmentStatus.CANCELLED
      : intent === "confirm"
        ? AppointmentStatus.CONFIRMED
        : AppointmentStatus.COMPLETED;

  const updated = await prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => {
    const updated = await transaction.appointment.updateMany({
      where: {
        id: appointment.id,
        professionalId,
        status:
          intent === "confirm"
            ? AppointmentStatus.PENDING
            : { in: activeAppointmentStatuses },
      },
      data:
        intent === "complete"
          ? {
              status: nextStatus,
              materialCost: appointment.service.materialCost,
            }
          : { status: nextStatus },
    });

    if (updated.count !== 1) {
      return false;
    }

    if (intent === "cancel") {
      await transaction.transaction.updateMany({
        where: {
          appointmentId: appointment.id,
          professionalId,
          type: "INCOME",
          status: "PENDING",
        },
        data: { status: "CANCELLED" },
      });
    }

    if (intent === "complete") {
      const description = `${appointment.service.name} · ${appointment.client.name}`;
      const paidAt = new Date();
      await transaction.transaction.upsert({
        where: {
          appointmentId_type: {
            appointmentId: appointment.id,
            type: TransactionType.INCOME,
          },
        },
        create: {
          professionalId,
          appointmentId: appointment.id,
          type: TransactionType.INCOME,
          status: TransactionStatus.PAID,
          amount: appointment.priceAtBooking,
          description,
          category: "Atendimento",
          dueAt: appointment.startsAt,
          paidAt,
        },
        update: {
          status: TransactionStatus.PAID,
          amount: appointment.priceAtBooking,
          paidAt,
        },
      });
      await transaction.transaction.upsert({
        where: {
          appointmentId_type: {
            appointmentId: appointment.id,
            type: TransactionType.EXPENSE,
          },
        },
        create: {
          professionalId,
          appointmentId: appointment.id,
          type: TransactionType.EXPENSE,
          status: TransactionStatus.PAID,
          amount: appointment.service.materialCost,
          description: `Materiais · ${description}`,
          category: "Custo Operacional",
          dueAt: appointment.startsAt,
          paidAt,
        },
        update: {
          status: TransactionStatus.PAID,
          amount: appointment.service.materialCost,
          paidAt,
        },
      });
    }

    return true;
  });

  if (!updated) {
    return { error: "O atendimento foi atualizado em outra operação." };
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/clients");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/finance");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/reports");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard");
  return {
    success:
      intent === "cancel"
        ? "Atendimento cancelado."
        : intent === "confirm"
          ? "Atendimento confirmado."
          : "Atendimento concluído.",
  };
}

export async function saveWorkingHoursRulesAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const rawStart = formData.get("startTime");
  const rawEnd = formData.get("endTime");
  const rawInterval = formData.get("interval");
  const rawBlockedSlots = formData.get("blockedSlots");
  const applyTo = formData.get("applyTo");
  const rawWeekday = formData.get("weekday");

  if (
    typeof rawStart !== "string" ||
    typeof rawEnd !== "string" ||
    typeof rawInterval !== "string" ||
    typeof rawBlockedSlots !== "string" ||
    (applyTo !== "all" && applyTo !== "day")
  ) {
    return { error: "As regras enviadas são inválidas." };
  }

  const minutePattern = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)$/;
  const startMatch = minutePattern.exec(rawStart);
  const endMatch = minutePattern.exec(rawEnd);
  const startMinute = startMatch
    ? Number(startMatch[1]) * 60 + Number(startMatch[2])
    : -1;
  const endMinute = endMatch
    ? Number(endMatch[1]) * 60 + Number(endMatch[2])
    : -1;
  const interval = Number(rawInterval);

  if (
    startMinute < 0 ||
    endMinute <= startMinute ||
    ![15, 30, 45, 60].includes(interval)
  ) {
    return { error: "Informe expediente e intervalo válidos." };
  }

  let blockedSlots: unknown;
  try {
    blockedSlots = JSON.parse(rawBlockedSlots);
  } catch {
    return { error: "A lista de pausas selecionadas é inválida." };
  }

  if (
    !Array.isArray(blockedSlots) ||
    !blockedSlots.every((slot): slot is string => typeof slot === "string")
  ) {
    return { error: "A lista de pausas selecionadas é inválida." };
  }

  const uniqueBlockedSlots = [...new Set(blockedSlots)];
  if (
    uniqueBlockedSlots.some((slot) => {
      const match = minutePattern.exec(slot);
      if (!match) return true;

      const minute = Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]);
      return (
        minute < startMinute ||
        minute + interval > endMinute ||
        (minute - startMinute) % interval !== 0
      );
    })
  ) {
    return {
      error: "As pausas precisam coincidir com os intervalos do expediente.",
    };
  }

  const rawActiveWeekdays = formData.getAll("activeWeekday");
  const activeWeekdays = rawActiveWeekdays.map((value) =>
    typeof value === "string" ? Number(value) : -1,
  );
  if (
    activeWeekdays.some(
      (weekday) => !Number.isInteger(weekday) || weekday < 0 || weekday > 6,
    )
  ) {
    return { error: "A seleção de dias de funcionamento é inválida." };
  }

  let weekdaysToSave: number[];
  if (applyTo === "all") {
    weekdaysToSave = [...new Set(activeWeekdays)];
    if (weekdaysToSave.length === 0) {
      return { error: "Selecione ao menos um dia de funcionamento." };
    }
  } else {
    const weekday = typeof rawWeekday === "string" ? Number(rawWeekday) : -1;
    if (!Number.isInteger(weekday) || weekday < 0 || weekday > 6) {
      return { error: "Selecione um dia da semana válido." };
    }
    weekdaysToSave = [weekday];
  }

  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const values = { startMinute, endMinute, interval, blockedSlots: uniqueBlockedSlots };
  await prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => {
    if (applyTo === "all") {
      await Promise.all(
        Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, weekday) => {
          const isActive = weekdaysToSave.includes(weekday);
          return transaction.workingHours.upsert({
            where: { professionalId_weekday: { professionalId, weekday } },
            create: {
              professionalId,
              weekday,
              ...values,
              isActive,
            },
            update: isActive ? { ...values, isActive: true } : { isActive: false },
          });
        }),
      );
      return;
    }

    const weekday = weekdaysToSave[0];
    await transaction.workingHours.upsert({
      where: { professionalId_weekday: { professionalId, weekday } },
      create: {
        professionalId,
        weekday,
        ...values,
        isActive: true,
      },
      update: { ...values, isActive: true },
    });
  });

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/agenda/configuracoes");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  return { success: "Regras de funcionamento salvas." };
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\actions\clients.ts ---
"use server";

import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";

function readClientFields(formData: FormData) {
  const name = formData.get("name");
  const whatsapp = formData.get("whatsapp");
  const preferredServiceId = formData.get("preferredServiceId");
  const notes = formData.get("notes");

  if (
    typeof name !== "string" ||
    typeof whatsapp !== "string" ||
    (preferredServiceId !== null && typeof preferredServiceId !== "string") ||
    (notes !== null && typeof notes !== "string")
  ) {
    return { error: "Revise os dados informados." } as const;
  }

  const normalizedName = name.trim();
  const normalizedWhatsapp = whatsapp.trim();
  const normalizedNotes = notes?.trim() || null;
  const normalizedServiceId = preferredServiceId?.trim() || null;
  const digits = normalizedWhatsapp.replace(/\D/g, "");

  if (normalizedName.length < 2 || normalizedName.length > 100) {
    return { error: "O nome deve ter entre 2 e 100 caracteres." } as const;
  }

  if (digits.length < 10 || digits.length > 15) {
    return { error: "Informe um WhatsApp com DDD e até 15 dígitos." } as const;
  }

  if ((normalizedNotes?.length ?? 0) > 1000) {
    return { error: "As observações devem ter no máximo 1.000 caracteres." } as const;
  }

  return {
    data: {
      name: normalizedName,
      whatsapp: normalizedWhatsapp,
      preferredServiceId: normalizedServiceId,
      notes: normalizedNotes,
    },
  } as const;
}

export async function saveClientAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const fields = readClientFields(formData);

  if ("error" in fields) {
    return fields;
  }

  const rawId = formData.get("clientId");
  const clientId = typeof rawId === "string" ? rawId.trim() : "";
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();

  if (fields.data.preferredServiceId) {
    const service = await prisma.service.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: fields.data.preferredServiceId,
        professionalId,
        isActive: true,
      },
      select: { id: true },
    });

    if (!service) {
      return { error: "O serviço preferido selecionado não está disponível." };
    }
  }

  if (clientId) {
    const result = await prisma.client.updateMany({
      where: { id: clientId, professionalId },
      data: fields.data,
    });

    if (result.count !== 1) {
      return { error: "Cliente não encontrado nesta conta." };
    }
  } else {
    await prisma.client.create({
      data: {
        ...fields.data,
        professionalId,
      },
    });
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/clients");
  return { success: clientId ? "Cliente atualizado." : "Cliente cadastrado." };
}

export async function deleteClientAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const rawId = formData.get("clientId");

  if (typeof rawId !== "string" || !rawId.trim()) {
    return { error: "Cliente inválido." };
  }

  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const client = await prisma.client.findFirst({
    where: { id: rawId, professionalId },
    select: {
      id: true,
      _count: { select: { appointments: true } },
    },
  });

  if (!client) {
    return { error: "Cliente não encontrado nesta conta." };
  }

  if (client._count.appointments > 0) {
    return {
      error:
        "Este cliente possui histórico de atendimentos. Para preservar o histórico, não é possível excluí-lo.",
    };
  }

  try {
    await prisma.client.deleteMany({
      where: { id: client.id, professionalId },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2003"
    ) {
      return {
        error:
          "Este cliente possui registros vinculados e não pode ser excluído.",
      };
    }

    throw error;
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/clients");
  return { success: "Cliente excluído." };
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\actions\finance.ts ---
"use server";

import {
  TransactionStatus,
  TransactionType,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { parseDateTimeInTimezone } from "@/lib/schedule";

export async function createTransactionAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const typeValue = formData.get("type");
  const descriptionValue = formData.get("description");
  const categoryValue = formData.get("category");
  const amountValue = formData.get("amount");
  const dueDateValue = formData.get("dueDate");
  const paid = formData.get("paid") === "on";

  if (
    (typeValue !== "INCOME" && typeValue !== "EXPENSE") ||
    typeof descriptionValue !== "string" ||
    typeof amountValue !== "string" ||
    (categoryValue !== null && typeof categoryValue !== "string") ||
    (dueDateValue !== null && typeof dueDateValue !== "string")
  ) {
    return { error: "Revise os dados do lançamento." };
  }

  const description = descriptionValue.trim();
  const category = categoryValue?.trim() || null;
  const amountPattern = /^\d{1,8}(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?$/;
  const normalizedAmount = amountValue.replace(",", ".");
  const numericAmount = Number(normalizedAmount);

  if (description.length < 2 || description.length > 120) {
    return { error: "A descrição deve ter entre 2 e 120 caracteres." };
  }

  if (
    category &&
    (category.length < 2 || category.length > 60)
  ) {
    return { error: "A categoria deve ter entre 2 e 60 caracteres." };
  }

  if (
    !amountPattern.test(amountValue) ||
    !Number.isFinite(numericAmount) ||
    numericAmount <= 0 ||
    numericAmount > 99_999_999.99
  ) {
    return { error: "Informe um valor maior que zero com até duas casas decimais." };
  }

  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUnique({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { timezone: true },
  });

  if (!professional) {
    return { error: "Profissional não encontrado." };
  }

  let dueAt: Date | null = null;
  if (dueDateValue) {
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dueDateValue)) {
      return { error: "Informe uma data válida para o lançamento." };
    }

    dueAt = parseDateTimeInTimezone(
      `${dueDateValue}T12:00`,
      professional.timezone,
    );

    if (!dueAt) {
      return { error: "Informe uma data válida para o lançamento." };
    }
  }

  await prisma.transaction.create({
    data: {
      professionalId,
      type:
        typeValue === "INCOME"
          ? TransactionType.INCOME
          : TransactionType.EXPENSE,
      status: paid ? TransactionStatus.PAID : TransactionStatus.PENDING,
      amount: normalizedAmount,
      description,
      category,
      dueAt,
      paidAt: paid ? new Date() : null,
    },
  });

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/finance");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/reports");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard");
  return { success: "Lançamento financeiro registrado." };
}

export async function updateTransactionStatusAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const transactionId = formData.get("transactionId");
  const nextStatusValue = formData.get("status");

  if (
    typeof transactionId !== "string" ||
    !transactionId.trim() ||
    (nextStatusValue !== "PAID" && nextStatusValue !== "CANCELLED")
  ) {
    return { error: "Ação financeira inválida." };
  }

  const transactionStatus =
    nextStatusValue === "PAID"
      ? TransactionStatus.PAID
      : TransactionStatus.CANCELLED;
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const current = await prisma.transaction.findFirst({
    where: { id: transactionId, professionalId },
    select: { id: true, status: true, appointmentId: true },
  });

  if (!current) {
    return { error: "Lançamento não encontrado nesta conta." };
  }

  if (current.status !== TransactionStatus.PENDING) {
    return { error: "Somente lançamentos pendentes podem ser atualizados." };
  }

  if (
    transactionStatus === TransactionStatus.CANCELLED &&
    current.appointmentId
  ) {
    return {
      error:
        "Para cancelar uma receita de atendimento, cancele o atendimento pela agenda.",
    };
  }

  const result = await prisma.transaction.updateMany({
    where: {
      id: current.id,
      professionalId,
      status: TransactionStatus.PENDING,
    },
    data: {
      status: transactionStatus,
      paidAt:
        transactionStatus === TransactionStatus.PAID ? new Date() : null,
    },
  });

  if (result.count !== 1) {
    return { error: "O lançamento foi atualizado em outra operação." };
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/finance");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/reports");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard");
  return {
    success:
      transactionStatus === TransactionStatus.PAID
        ? "Pagamento registrado."
        : "Lançamento cancelado.",
  };
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\actions\onboarding.ts ---
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function completeWelcomeTourAction(
  previousState: FormState,
): Promise<FormState> {
  void previousState;
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const result = await getPrismaClient().professional.updateMany({
    where: { id: professionalId, hasSeenWelcome: false },
    data: { hasSeenWelcome: true },
  });

  if (result.count === 0) {
    const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUnique({
      where: { id: professionalId },
      select: { id: true },
    });
    if (!professional) {
      return { error: "Não foi possível localizar sua conta. Entre novamente." };
    }
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard");
  return { success: "Boas-vindas concluídas." };
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\actions\public-booking.ts ---
"use server";

import {
  AppointmentStatus,
  Prisma,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { getDayBounds, isValidBookingDate } from "@/lib/public-booking";
import {
  getDateInTimezone,
  getWeekdayInTimezone,
  isAvailableWorkingHoursSlot,
  parseDateTimeInTimezone,
} from "@/lib/schedule";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";

class BookingUnavailableError extends Error {}

const activeAppointmentStatuses: AppointmentStatus[] = [
  AppointmentStatus.PENDING,
  AppointmentStatus.CONFIRMED,
];

export async function createPublicBookingAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const slug = formData.get("slug");
  const serviceId = formData.get("serviceId");
  const date = formData.get("date");
  const startsAtInput = formData.get("startsAt");
  const nameInput = formData.get("name");
  const whatsappInput = formData.get("whatsapp");

  if (
    typeof slug !== "string" ||
    typeof serviceId !== "string" ||
    typeof date !== "string" ||
    typeof startsAtInput !== "string" ||
    typeof nameInput !== "string" ||
    typeof whatsappInput !== "string"
  ) {
    return { error: "Preencha os campos obrigatórios do agendamento." };
  }

  const name = nameInput.trim();
  const whatsapp = whatsappInput.replace(/\D/g, "");

  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 100) {
    return { error: "O nome deve ter entre 2 e 100 caracteres." };
  }

  if (whatsapp.length < 10 || whatsapp.length > 15) {
    return { error: "Informe um WhatsApp com DDD e até 15 dígitos." };
  }

  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUnique({
    where: { slug },
    select: { id: true, timezone: true, pixKey: true, expiresAt: true },
  });

  if (!professional) {
    return { error: "Este link de agendamento não está disponível." };
  }

  if (professional.expiresAt <= new Date()) {
    return {
      error:
        "Este profissional está temporariamente indisponível para agendamentos online.",
    };
  }

  if (!isValidBookingDate(date, professional.timezone)) {
    return { error: "Escolha uma data disponível nos próximos 90 dias." };
  }

  const startsAt = parseDateTimeInTimezone(
    startsAtInput,
    professional.timezone,
  );

  if (
    !startsAt ||
    startsAt <= new Date() ||
    getDateInTimezone(startsAt, professional.timezone) !== date
  ) {
    return { error: "O horário escolhido não é válido." };
  }

  const service = await prisma.service.findFirst({
    where: {
      id: serviceId,
      professionalId: professional.id,
      isActive: true,
      onlineBookingEnabled: true,
    },
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      price: true,
      durationMinutes: true,
      depositAmount: true,
    },
  });

  if (!service) {
    return { error: "Este serviço não está disponível para agendamento online." };
  }

  if (Number(service.depositAmount ?? 0) > 0 && !professional.pixKey) {
    return {
      error:
        "O sinal deste serviço está temporariamente indisponível. Entre em contato com a profissional.",
    };
  }

  const endsAt = new Date(
    startsAt.getTime() + service.durationMinutes * 60_000,
  );
  const bounds = getDayBounds(date, professional.timezone);

  if (
    !bounds ||
    startsAt < bounds.start ||
    endsAt > bounds.end
  ) {
    return { error: "O horário escolhido não é válido." };
  }

  const workingHours = await prisma.workingHours.findUnique({
    where: {
      professionalId_weekday: {
        professionalId: professional.id,
        weekday: getWeekdayInTimezone(startsAt, professional.timezone),
      },
    },
  });

  const localStartParts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone: professional.timezone,
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    hourCycle: "h23",
  }).formatToParts(startsAt);
  const localEndParts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone: professional.timezone,
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    hourCycle: "h23",
  }).formatToParts(endsAt);
  const startValues = Object.fromEntries(
    localStartParts.map((part) => [part.type, part.value]),
  );
  const endValues = Object.fromEntries(
    localEndParts.map((part) => [part.type, part.value]),
  );
  const startMinute =
    Number(startValues.hour) * 60 + Number(startValues.minute);
  const endMinute = Number(endValues.hour) * 60 + Number(endValues.minute);
  if (
    !workingHours?.isActive ||
    !isAvailableWorkingHoursSlot(startMinute, endMinute, workingHours)
  ) {
    return {
      error: "O horário escolhido não está mais disponível ou coincide com uma pausa.",
    };
  }

  const rawNotes = formData.get("notes");
  if (rawNotes !== null && typeof rawNotes !== "string") {
    return { error: "Observações inválidas." };
  }

  const notes =
    typeof rawNotes === "string" ? rawNotes.trim().slice(0, 500) || null : null;

  let appointmentId: string;
  try {
    appointmentId = await prisma.$transaction(
      async (transaction) => {
        const [appointmentConflict, blockConflict] = await Promise.all([
          transaction.appointment.findFirst({
            where: {
              professionalId: professional.id,
              status: { in: activeAppointmentStatuses },
              startsAt: { lt: endsAt },
              endsAt: { gt: startsAt },
            },
            select: { id: true },
          }),
          transaction.calendarBlock.findFirst({
            where: {
              professionalId: professional.id,
              startsAt: { lt: endsAt },
              endsAt: { gt: startsAt },
            },
            select: { id: true },
          }),
        ]);

        if (appointmentConflict || blockConflict) {
          throw new BookingUnavailableError(
            "Este horário acabou de ser reservado. Escolha outro horário.",
          );
        }

        const existingClients = await transaction.client.findMany({
          where: { professionalId: professional.id },
          select: { id: true, name: true, whatsapp: true },
        });
        const existingClientByWhatsapp = existingClients.find(
          (client) => client.whatsapp.replace(/\D/g, "") === whatsapp,
        );
        const normalizedName = name.toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        const existingClientByName = existingClients.find(
          (client) =>
            client.name
              .trim()
              .toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR")
              .replace(/\s+/g, " ") === normalizedName,
        );
        const existingClient =
          existingClientByWhatsapp ?? existingClientByName;
        const client = existingClient
          ? { id: existingClient.id, name: existingClient.name }
          : await transaction.client.create({
              data: {
                professionalId: professional.id,
                name,
                whatsapp,
              },
              select: { id: true, name: true },
            });

        const appointment = await transaction.appointment.create({
          data: {
            professionalId: professional.id,
            clientId: client.id,
            serviceId: service.id,
            startsAt,
            endsAt,
            priceAtBooking: service.price,
            depositAmount: service.depositAmount ?? 0,
            status: AppointmentStatus.PENDING,
            notes,
          },
          select: { id: true },
        });

        await transaction.transaction.create({
          data: {
            professionalId: professional.id,
            appointmentId: appointment.id,
            type: "INCOME",
            status: "PENDING",
            amount: service.price,
            description: `${service.name} · ${client.name}`,
            category: "Agendamento online",
            dueAt: startsAt,
          },
        });
        return appointment.id;
      },
      { isolationLevel: Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof BookingUnavailableError) {
      return { error: error.message };
    }

    if (
      error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2034"
    ) {
      return {
        error:
          "A agenda acabou de ser alterada. Atualize os horários e tente novamente.",
      };
    }

    throw error;
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/clients");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/finance");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/reports");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard");
  const search = new URLSearchParams({
    success: "1",
    appointmentId,
  });
  redirect(
    `/agendar/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}?${search.toString()}`,
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\actions\services.ts ---
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";

export async function saveServiceAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const name = formData.get("name");
  const description = formData.get("description");
  const price = formData.get("price");
  const materialCost = formData.get("materialCost");
  const depositAmount = formData.get("depositAmount");
  const durationMinutes = formData.get("durationMinutes");
  const serviceId = formData.get("serviceId");

  if (
    typeof name !== "string" ||
    (description !== null && typeof description !== "string") ||
    typeof price !== "string" ||
    typeof materialCost !== "string" ||
    (depositAmount !== null && typeof depositAmount !== "string") ||
    typeof durationMinutes !== "string" ||
    (serviceId !== null && typeof serviceId !== "string")
  ) {
    return { error: "Revise os dados do serviço." };
  }

  const normalizedName = name.trim();
  const normalizedDescription = description?.trim() || null;
  const pricePattern = /^(?:\d{1,7})(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?$/;
  const normalizedPrice = price.replace(",", ".");
  const normalizedMaterialCost = materialCost.replace(",", ".");
  const normalizedDepositAmount =
    typeof depositAmount === "string" && depositAmount.trim()
      ? depositAmount.replace(",", ".")
      : "0";
  const parsedDuration = Number(durationMinutes);
  const isUpdate = typeof serviceId === "string" && Boolean(serviceId.trim());

  if (normalizedName.length < 2 || normalizedName.length > 100) {
    return { error: "O nome do serviço deve ter entre 2 e 100 caracteres." };
  }

  if (!pricePattern.test(price) || Number(normalizedPrice) <= 0) {
    return { error: "Informe um preço maior que zero, com até duas casas decimais." };
  }

  if (
    !/^(?:\d{1,8})(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?$/.test(materialCost) ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(normalizedMaterialCost)) ||
    Number(normalizedMaterialCost) > 99_999_999.99
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Informe um custo de material válido, igual ou maior que zero e com até duas casas decimais.",
    };
  }

  const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUnique({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { pixKey: true },
  });
  if (!professional) {
    return { error: "Profissional não encontrado." };
  }

  if (
    !/^(?:\d{1,8})(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?$/.test(normalizedDepositAmount) ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(normalizedDepositAmount)) ||
    Number(normalizedDepositAmount) < 0 ||
    Number(normalizedDepositAmount) > Number(normalizedPrice)
  ) {
    return {
      error: "O valor do sinal deve ser igual ou maior que zero e não pode superar o preço do serviço.",
    };
  }

  if (!professional.pixKey && Number(normalizedDepositAmount) > 0) {
    return {
      error: "Cadastre sua chave PIX antes de configurar um sinal para o serviço.",
    };
  }

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(parsedDuration) ||
    parsedDuration < 5 ||
    parsedDuration > 1440
  ) {
    return { error: "A duração deve ser um número entre 5 e 1.440 minutos." };
  }

  if ((normalizedDescription?.length ?? 0) > 500) {
    return { error: "A descrição deve ter no máximo 500 caracteres." };
  }

  const data = {
    name: normalizedName,
    description: normalizedDescription,
    price: normalizedPrice,
    materialCost: normalizedMaterialCost,
    depositAmount: normalizedDepositAmount,
    durationMinutes: parsedDuration,
    onlineBookingEnabled: formData.get("onlineBookingEnabled") === "on",
    isActive: isUpdate ? formData.get("isActive") === "on" : true,
  };
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();

  if (isUpdate && typeof serviceId === "string") {
    const result = await prisma.service.updateMany({
      where: { id: serviceId.trim(), professionalId },
      data,
    });

    if (result.count !== 1) {
      return { error: "Serviço não encontrado nesta conta." };
    }
  } else {
    await prisma.service.create({
      data: {
        ...data,
        professionalId,
      },
    });
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/services");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/clients");
  return { success: serviceId ? "Serviço atualizado." : "Serviço cadastrado." };
}

export async function savePixKeyAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const pixKeyValue = formData.get("pixKey");

  if (typeof pixKeyValue !== "string") {
    return { error: "Informe uma chave PIX válida." };
  }

  const pixKey = pixKeyValue.trim();
  if (pixKey.length < 3 || pixKey.length > 200) {
    return { error: "A chave PIX deve ter entre 3 e 200 caracteres." };
  }

  await getPrismaClient().professional.update({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    data: { pixKey },
  });

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/services");
  revalidatePath("/agendar/[slug]", "page");
  return { success: "Chave PIX salva." };
}

export async function toggleServiceActiveAction(
  _previousState: FormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId();
  const serviceId = formData.get("serviceId");
  const active = formData.get("active");

  if (
    typeof serviceId !== "string" ||
    !serviceId.trim() ||
    (active !== "true" && active !== "false")
  ) {
    return { error: "Serviço inválido." };
  }

  const result = await getPrismaClient().service.updateMany({
    where: { id: serviceId, professionalId },
    data: { isActive: active === "true" },
  });

  if (result.count !== 1) {
    return { error: "Serviço não encontrado nesta conta." };
  }

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/services");
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/calendar");
  return { success: active === "true" ? "Serviço reativado." : "Serviço desativado." };
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\actions\subscriptions.ts ---
"use server";

import { MercadoPagoConfig, Preference } from "mercadopago";
import {
  isSubscriptionPlanType,
  subscriptionPlans,
} from "@/lib/subscriptions";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";

type SubscriptionCheckoutState = {
  error?: string;
  initUrl?: string;
};

function serializeErrorDetails(error: unknown): string {
  const seen = new WeakSet<object>();

  return (
    JSON.stringify(
      { error },
      (_key, value: unknown) => {
        if (typeof value === "bigint") {
          return value.toString();
        }

        if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null) {
          if (seen.has(value)) {
            return "[Circular]";
          }
          seen.add(value);

          if (value instanceof Error) {
            return Object.fromEntries(
              Object.getOwnPropertyNames(value).map((property) => [
                property,
                Reflect.get(value, property),
              ]),
            );
          }
        }

        return value;
      },
      2,
    ) ?? String(error)
  );
}

function getApplicationUrl(): string {
  const configuredUrl =
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL ??
    (process.env.VERCEL_URL ? `https://${process.env.VERCEL_URL}` : null) ??
    (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"
      ? "http://localhost:3000"
      : null);

  if (!configuredUrl) {
    throw new Error("NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL is required for subscription checkout.");
  }

  const url = new URL(configuredUrl);
  if (url.protocol !== "https:" && process.env.NODE_ENV !== "development") {
    throw new Error("Subscription checkout requires an HTTPS application URL.");
  }

  return url.origin;
}

export async function createSubscriptionCheckoutAction(
  _previousState: SubscriptionCheckoutState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<SubscriptionCheckoutState> {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const planTypeValue = formData.get("planType");

  if (
    typeof planTypeValue !== "string" ||
    !isSubscriptionPlanType(planTypeValue)
  ) {
    return { error: "Selecione um plano válido." };
  }

  const accessToken = process.env.MERCADOPAGO_ACCESS_TOKEN;
  if (!accessToken) {
    return {
      error: "O checkout está temporariamente indisponível. Tente novamente mais tarde.",
    };
  }

  const plan = subscriptionPlans[planTypeValue];
  const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUnique({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { email: true },
  });

  if (!professional) {
    return { error: "Profissional não encontrado." };
  }

  try {
    const applicationUrl = getApplicationUrl();
    const preference = new Preference(
      new MercadoPagoConfig({ accessToken }),
    );
    const body = {
      items: [
        {
          id: `vitta-plan-${planTypeValue.toLowerCase()}`,
          title: `Vitta Beauty - Plano ${plan.name}`,
          quantity: 1,
          unit_price: plan.amount,
          currency_id: "BRL",
        },
      ],
      payer: { email: professional.email },
      back_urls: {
        success:
          "https://vittabeauty.vercel.app/dashboard/conta/assinatura?status=success",
        failure:
          "https://vittabeauty.vercel.app/dashboard/conta/assinatura?status=failure",
        pending:
          "https://vittabeauty.vercel.app/dashboard/conta/assinatura?status=pending",
      },
      auto_return: "approved",
      external_reference: `${professionalId}:${planTypeValue}`,
      notification_url: `${applicationUrl}/api/webhooks/mercadopago`,
    } satisfies Parameters<Preference["create"]>[0]["body"];
    console.log("Payload enviado ao MP:", JSON.stringify(body, null, 2));
    const checkoutPreference = await preference.create({
      body,
    });

    const initUrl =
      checkoutPreference.init_point ?? checkoutPreference.sandbox_init_point;
    if (!initUrl) {
      throw new Error("Mercado Pago did not return checkout details.");
    }

    return { initUrl };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "[Mercado Pago] Falha detalhada ao criar assinatura:",
      serializeErrorDetails(error),
    );
    return {
      error:
        error instanceof Error && error.message.trim()
          ? error.message
          : "Não foi possível iniciar o checkout. Tente novamente em instantes.",
    };
  }
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\admin\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-access";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Administração",
};

type AdminPageProps = {
  searchParams: Promise<{
    from?: string;
    to?: string;
  }>;
};

function isDateInput(value: string | undefined): value is string {
  if (!value || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) return false;
  const parsed = new Date(`${value}T12:00:00-03:00`);
  return !Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) && parsed.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value;
}

function formatCurrency(value: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "BRL",
  }).format(value);
}

function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
  if (!value) return "Não informado";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    dateStyle: "short",
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
  }).format(value);
}

const paymentStatusLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  approved: "Aprovado",
  pending: "Pendente",
  in_process: "Em processamento",
  rejected: "Recusado",
  cancelled: "Cancelado",
  refunded: "Reembolsado",
  charged_back: "Contestado",
};

const planLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  MONTHLY: "Mensal",
  SEMIANNUAL: "Semestral",
  ANNUAL: "Anual",
  FREE: "Gratuito",
};

export default async function AdminPage({ searchParams }: AdminPageProps) {
  const actorProfessionalId = await requireAdmin();
  const query = await searchParams;
  const now = new Date();
  const defaultFrom = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1)
    .toISOString()
    .slice(0, 10);
  const defaultTo = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const from = isDateInput(query.from) ? query.from : defaultFrom;
  const to = isDateInput(query.to) ? query.to : defaultTo;
  if (from > to) {
    redirect("/admin");
  }

  const rangeStart = new Date(`${from}T00:00:00-03:00`);
  const rangeEnd = new Date(`${to}T23:59:59.999-03:00`);
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professionalOnly = { role: "PROFESSIONAL" as const };
  const professionalPaymentsOnly = { professional: professionalOnly };

  await prisma.adminAuditLog.create({
    data: {
      actorProfessionalId,
      action: "VIEW_ADMIN_OVERVIEW",
    },
  });

  const [
    totalProfessionals,
    activeProfessionals,
    trialProfessionals,
    expiredProfessionals,
    newProfessionals,
    paidPayments,
    paymentsWithoutAmount,
    pendingPayments,
    refundedPayments,
    recentProfessionals,
    recentPayments,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.professional.count({ where: professionalOnly }),
    prisma.professional.count({
      where: { ...professionalOnly, expiresAt: { gt: now } },
    }),
    prisma.professional.count({
      where: {
        ...professionalOnly,
        subscriptionStatus: "TRIAL",
        expiresAt: { gt: now },
      },
    }),
    prisma.professional.count({
      where: { ...professionalOnly, expiresAt: { lte: now } },
    }),
    prisma.professional.count({
      where: {
        ...professionalOnly,
        createdAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd },
      },
    }),
    prisma.subscriptionPayment.aggregate({
      where: {
        ...professionalPaymentsOnly,
        status: "approved",
        paidAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd },
      },
      _sum: { amount: true, refundedAmount: true },
      _count: { _all: true },
    }),
    prisma.subscriptionPayment.count({
      where: {
        ...professionalPaymentsOnly,
        status: "approved",
        amount: null,
        paidAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd },
      },
    }),
    prisma.subscriptionPayment.count({
      where: {
        ...professionalPaymentsOnly,
        status: { in: ["pending", "in_process"] },
        OR: [
          { paidAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd } },
          { createdAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd } },
        ],
      },
    }),
    prisma.subscriptionPayment.count({
      where: {
        ...professionalPaymentsOnly,
        status: { in: ["refunded", "charged_back"] },
        OR: [
          { paidAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd } },
          { createdAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd } },
        ],
      },
    }),
    prisma.professional.findMany({
      where: professionalOnly,
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      take: 100,
      select: {
        id: true,
        name: true,
        email: true,
        createdAt: true,
        expiresAt: true,
        subscriptionStatus: true,
        planType: true,
        role: true,
      },
    }),
    prisma.subscriptionPayment.findMany({
      where: {
        ...professionalPaymentsOnly,
        OR: [
          { paidAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd } },
          { createdAt: { gte: rangeStart, lte: rangeEnd } },
        ],
      },
      orderBy: [{ paidAt: "desc" }, { createdAt: "desc" }],
      take: 100,
      select: {
        mercadoPagoPaymentId: true,
        planType: true,
        amount: true,
        currency: true,
        paymentMethod: true,
        paymentType: true,
        status: true,
        statusDetail: true,
        refundedAmount: true,
        paidAt: true,
        professional: { select: { name: true, email: true } },
      },
    }),
  ]);
  const revenue =
    (paidPayments._sum.amount?.toNumber() ?? 0) -
    (paidPayments._sum.refundedAmount?.toNumber() ?? 0);
  const unknownHistoricalCount = paymentsWithoutAmount;

  return (
    <section>
      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.17em] text-vitta">
            Indicadores da plataforma
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text">
            Visão geral
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
            Painel administrativo somente para consulta.
          </p>
        </div>
        <form className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3" method="get">
          <label className="grid gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-muted">
            De
            <input
              className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-vitta-text"
              defaultValue={from}
              name="from"
              type="date"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="grid gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-muted">
            Até
            <input
              className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-vitta-text"
              defaultValue={to}
              name="to"
              type="date"
            />
          </label>
          <button
            className="rounded-lg bg-vitta px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-vitta/90"
            type="submit"
          >
            Filtrar
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
        {[
          { label: "Receita líquida confirmada", value: formatCurrency(revenue) },
          { label: "Pagamentos aprovados", value: String(paidPayments._count._all) },
          { label: "Profissionais", value: String(totalProfessionals) },
          { label: "Acesso vigente", value: String(activeProfessionals) },
          { label: "Trials vigentes", value: String(trialProfessionals) },
          { label: "Acessos expirados", value: String(expiredProfessionals) },
          { label: "Novos cadastros no período", value: String(newProfessionals) },
          { label: "Pagamentos pendentes", value: String(pendingPayments) },
          { label: "Pagamentos reembolsados/contestados", value: String(refundedPayments) },
        ].map((metric) => (
          <article
            className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-4 shadow-card"
            key={metric.label}
          >
            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-vitta-muted">{metric.label}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text">
              {metric.value}
            </p>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
      {unknownHistoricalCount > 0 && (
        <p className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-amber-950">
          Existem {unknownHistoricalCount} pagamentos aprovados neste período
          sem valor salvo no banco. Eles são registros históricos anteriores ao
          detalhamento do pagamento e não entram no total de receita.
        </p>
      )}

      <section className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white shadow-card">
        <div className="border-b border-vitta-border px-5 py-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Profissionais
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
            Exibindo as 100 contas mais recentes. Nenhuma ação destrutiva está disponível.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[780px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-[#faf7fb] text-xs text-vitta-muted">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Profissional</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Cadastro</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Plano</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Status</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Expiração</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Acesso</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-vitta-border">
              {recentProfessionals.map((professional) => (
                <tr key={professional.id}>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3">
                    <p className="font-medium text-vitta-text">
                      {professional.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                      {professional.email}
                    </p>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-muted">
                    {formatDate(professional.createdAt)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-text">
                    {planLabels[professional.planType] ?? professional.planType}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-text">
                    {professional.subscriptionStatus}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-muted">
                    {formatDate(professional.expiresAt)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3">
                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${
                        professional.expiresAt > now
                          ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800"
                          : "bg-red-50 text-red-800"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {professional.expiresAt > now ? "Vigente" : "Expirado"}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white shadow-card">
        <div className="border-b border-vitta-border px-5 py-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Pagamentos do Mercado Pago
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
            Até 100 registros criados ou pagos no período selecionado.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[900px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-[#faf7fb] text-xs text-vitta-muted">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Pagamento</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Profissional</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Plano</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Valor</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Meio</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Status</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-3 font-semibold">Data</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-vitta-border">
              {recentPayments.map((payment) => (
                <tr key={payment.mercadoPagoPaymentId}>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                    {payment.mercadoPagoPaymentId}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3">
                    <p className="font-medium text-vitta-text">
                      {payment.professional.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                      {payment.professional.email}
                    </p>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-text">
                    {planLabels[payment.planType] ?? payment.planType}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-text">
                    {payment.amount
                      ? formatCurrency(payment.amount.toNumber())
                      : "Valor indisponível"}
                    {payment.refundedAmount &&
                      payment.refundedAmount.toNumber() > 0 && (
                        <span className="block text-xs text-red-700">
                          Estornado:{" "}
                          {formatCurrency(payment.refundedAmount.toNumber())}
                        </span>
                      )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-muted">
                    {[payment.paymentType, payment.paymentMethod]
                      .filter(Boolean)
                      .join(" · ") || "Não informado"}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-text">
                    {paymentStatusLabels[payment.status] ?? payment.status}
                    {payment.statusDetail && (
                      <span className="block text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                        {payment.statusDetail}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-vitta-muted">
                    {formatDate(payment.paidAt)}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
              {recentPayments.length === 0 && (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-vitta-muted"
                    colSpan={7}
                  >
                    Nenhum pagamento com data no período selecionado.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>
      <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
        Os valores representam pagamentos processados pelo Vitta Beauty, não o
        faturamento dos atendimentos dos profissionais.
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\agendar\[slug]\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Image from "next/image";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { PublicBookingForm } from "@/components/booking/public-booking-form";
import {
  buildAvailableSlots,
  getDayBounds,
  isValidBookingDate,
} from "@/lib/public-booking";
import {
  getDateInTimezone,
  getWeekdayInTimezone,
  parseDateTimeInTimezone,
} from "@/lib/schedule";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { AppointmentStatus } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { getWhatsAppNumber } from "@/lib/whatsapp";

type BookingPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{
    date?: string;
    serviceId?: string;
    success?: string;
    appointmentId?: string;
  }>;
};

const currency = new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
  style: "currency",
  currency: "BRL",
});

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: Pick<BookingPageProps, "params">): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUnique({
    where: { slug },
    select: { name: true },
  });

  return {
    title: professional
      ? `Agendar com ${professional.name}`
      : "Agendamento online",
    description: professional
      ? `Consulte os serviços e horários de ${professional.name}.`
      : "Agendamento online Vitta Beauty.",
  };
}

export default async function PublicBookingPage({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: BookingPageProps) {
  const [{ slug }, query] = await Promise.all([params, searchParams]);
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUnique({
    where: { slug },
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      timezone: true,
      expiresAt: true,
      pixKey: true,
      phone: true,
      services: {
        where: { isActive: true, onlineBookingEnabled: true },
        orderBy: { name: "asc" },
        select: {
          id: true,
          name: true,
          description: true,
          price: true,
          depositAmount: true,
          durationMinutes: true,
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (!professional) {
    notFound();
  }

  if (professional.expiresAt <= new Date()) {
    return (
      <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-2xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 sm:py-20">
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-card sm:p-12">
          <Image
            alt="Vitta Beauty"
            className="mx-auto h-[76px] w-[170px] object-contain"
            height={360}
            priority
            src="/brand/logo_vitta.png"
            width={640}
          />
          <h1 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Agendamentos temporariamente indisponíveis
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
            Este profissional está temporariamente indisponível para
            agendamentos online.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const successfulBooking =
    query.success === "1" && query.appointmentId
      ? await prisma.appointment.findFirst({
          where: {
            id: query.appointmentId,
            professionalId: professional.id,
          },
          select: {
            startsAt: true,
            depositAmount: true,
            service: { select: { name: true } },
          },
        })
      : null;
  const bookingDateTime = successfulBooking
    ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
        timeZone: professional.timezone,
        dateStyle: "full",
        timeStyle: "short",
      }).format(successfulBooking.startsAt)
    : null;
  const professionalWhatsApp = getWhatsAppNumber(professional.phone ?? "");
  const depositValue = Number(successfulBooking?.depositAmount ?? 0);
  const whatsappMessage = successfulBooking && bookingDateTime
    ? `Olá! Acabei de solicitar o serviço ${successfulBooking.service.name} para ${bookingDateTime} no Vitta Beauty. Segue o comprovante do sinal de ${currency.format(depositValue)}.`
    : "";
  const whatsappHref =
    successfulBooking &&
    depositValue > 0 &&
    professionalWhatsApp.length >= 12
      ? `https://wa.me/${professionalWhatsApp}?text=${encodeURIComponent(whatsappMessage)}`
      : null;

  const today = getDateInTimezone(new Date(), professional.timezone);
  const date = query.date ?? today;
  const selectedService =
    professional.services.find((service) => service.id === query.serviceId) ??
    null;
  const validDate = isValidBookingDate(date, professional.timezone);
  let slots: ReturnType<typeof buildAvailableSlots> = [];

  if (selectedService && validDate) {
    const bounds = getDayBounds(date, professional.timezone);

    if (bounds) {
      const localNoon = parseDateTimeInTimezone(
        `${date}T12:00`,
        professional.timezone,
      );

      if (!localNoon) {
        throw new Error(
          `Could not resolve local noon for timezone "${professional.timezone}".`,
        );
      }

      const weekdayIndex = getWeekdayInTimezone(
        localNoon,
        professional.timezone,
      );

      const [workingHours, appointments, blocks] = await Promise.all([
        prisma.workingHours.findUnique({
          where: {
            professionalId_weekday: {
              professionalId: professional.id,
              weekday: weekdayIndex,
            },
          },
        }),
        prisma.appointment.findMany({
          where: {
            professionalId: professional.id,
            status: {
              in: [AppointmentStatus.PENDING, AppointmentStatus.CONFIRMED],
            },
            startsAt: { lt: bounds.end },
            endsAt: { gt: bounds.start },
          },
          select: { startsAt: true, endsAt: true },
        }),
        prisma.calendarBlock.findMany({
          where: {
            professionalId: professional.id,
            startsAt: { lt: bounds.end },
            endsAt: { gt: bounds.start },
          },
          select: { startsAt: true, endsAt: true },
        }),
      ]);

      slots = buildAvailableSlots({
        date,
        timeZone: professional.timezone,
        durationMinutes: selectedService.durationMinutes,
        workingHours,
        appointments,
        blocks,
      });
    }
  }

  const maxDate = new Date(`${today}T00:00:00.000Z`);
  maxDate.setUTCDate(maxDate.getUTCDate() + 90);
  const dateMax = maxDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const formattedDate = validDate
    ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
        timeZone: "UTC",
        dateStyle: "full",
      }).format(new Date(`${date}T12:00:00.000Z`))
    : null;

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-12">
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white shadow-card">
        <header className="bg-vitta-background px-6 py-8 text-center sm:px-10">
          <Image
            alt="Vitta Beauty"
            className="mx-auto h-[76px] w-[170px] object-contain"
            height={360}
            priority
            src="/brand/logo_vitta.png"
            width={640}
          />
          <h1 className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
            Agende seu horário
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-vitta-muted">
            com{" "}
            <span className="break-words font-semibold text-vitta-text">
              {professional.name}
            </span>
          </p>
        </header>

        <div className="space-y-6 p-5 sm:p-8">
          {query.success === "1" && (
            <section
              className="space-y-4 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-emerald-950"
              role="status"
            >
              <p>
                Sua solicitação foi enviada.
              </p>
              {successfulBooking && depositValue > 0 && (
                <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-emerald-200 pt-4">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="font-semibold">
                      Sinal necessário para confirmar seu agendamento
                    </h2>
                    <p className="mt-1">
                      Envie o sinal de{" "}
                      <strong>{currency.format(depositValue)}</strong> via PIX
                      para a chave:
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-2 break-all rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 bg-white/80 px-3 py-2 font-semibold">
                      {professional.pixKey}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {whatsappHref ? (
                    <a
                      className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#25D366] px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#1fb95a]"
                      href={whatsappHref}
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      target="_blank"
                    >
                      Enviar comprovante pelo WhatsApp
                    </a>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="text-xs text-emerald-900">
                      Após o pagamento, envie o comprovante diretamente para a
                      profissional pelo WhatsApp.
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          )}

          {professional.services.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-vitta-background p-5 text-center">
              <h2 className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                Agendamentos online indisponíveis
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                No momento, não há serviços disponíveis para agendamento online.
                Entre em contato diretamente com a profissional.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <form
                action={`/agendar/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`}
                className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto]"
              >
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
                  Serviço
                  <select
                    className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-3 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
                    defaultValue={selectedService?.id ?? ""}
                    name="serviceId"
                    required
                  >
                    <option disabled value="">
                      Selecione um serviço
                    </option>
                    {professional.services.map((service) => (
                      <option key={service.id} value={service.id}>
                        {service.name} · {currency.format(Number(service.price))} ·{" "}
                        {service.durationMinutes} min
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
                  Data
                  <input
                    className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-3 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
                    defaultValue={date}
                    max={dateMax}
                    min={today}
                    name="date"
                    required
                    type="date"
                  />
                </label>
                <button
                  className="rounded-xl border border-vitta px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta transition hover:bg-vitta/5 sm:col-span-2"
                  type="submit"
                >
                  Consultar horários
                </button>
              </form>

              {selectedService && (
                <section className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border p-4 sm:p-5">
                  <div className="mb-5">
                    <h2 className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                      {selectedService.name}
                    </h2>
                    {selectedService.description && (
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
                        {selectedService.description}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta">
                      {currency.format(Number(selectedService.price))} ·{" "}
                      {selectedService.durationMinutes} minutos
                    </p>
                    {Number(selectedService.depositAmount ?? 0) > 0 && (
                      <p className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-vitta-background px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
                        Sinal para solicitar:{" "}
                        {currency.format(Number(selectedService.depositAmount))}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {formattedDate && (
                      <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-medium capitalize text-vitta-text">
                        {formattedDate}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {!validDate ? (
                    <p className="rounded-xl bg-vitta-background p-4 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                      Escolha uma data entre hoje e os próximos 90 dias para
                      consultar a disponibilidade.
                    </p>
                  ) : (
                    <PublicBookingForm
                      date={date}
                      serviceId={selectedService.id}
                      slug={slug}
                      slots={slots}
                      timeZone={professional.timezone}
                    />
                  )}
                </section>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
