--- ARQUIVO: src\app\api\webhooks\mercadopago\route.ts ---
import {
  Invoice,
  MercadoPagoConfig,
  Payment,
  PreApproval,
  WebhookSignatureValidator,
} from "mercadopago";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { syncCheckoutPayment } from "@/lib/subscription-payments";
import {
  isSubscriptionPlanType,
  subscriptionPlans,
} from "@/lib/subscriptions";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

type WebhookPayload = {
  type?: unknown;
  action?: unknown;
  data?: { id?: unknown };
};

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function getPlanReference(value: string | undefined) {
  if (!value) return null;
  const separator = value.lastIndexOf(":");
  if (separator <= 0) return null;

  const professionalId = value.slice(0, separator);
  const planType = value.slice(separator + 1);
  if (!professionalId || !isSubscriptionPlanType(planType)) return null;
  return { professionalId, planType };
}

function getClient() {
  const accessToken = process.env.MERCADOPAGO_ACCESS_TOKEN;
  if (!accessToken) {
    throw new Error("MERCADOPAGO_ACCESS_TOKEN is not configured.");
  }
  return new MercadoPagoConfig({ accessToken });
}

async function handlePreapproval(preapprovalId: string): Promise<void> {
  const preapproval = await new PreApproval(getClient()).get({
    id: preapprovalId,
  });
  const reference = getPlanReference(preapproval.external_reference);
  if (
    !reference ||
    (preapproval.status !== "cancelled" && preapproval.status !== "canceled")
  ) {
    return;
  }

  await getPrismaClient().professional.updateMany({
    where: {
      id: reference.professionalId,
      mpSubscriptionId: preapprovalId,
    },
    data: { subscriptionStatus: "CANCELED" },
  });
}

async function handleAuthorizedPayment(paymentId: string): Promise<void> {
  const invoice = await new Invoice(getClient()).get({ id: paymentId });
  if (!invoice.preapproval_id) return;

  const preapproval = await new PreApproval(getClient()).get({
    id: invoice.preapproval_id,
  });
  const reference = getPlanReference(preapproval.external_reference);
  if (!reference) return;

  const paymentStatus = invoice.payment?.status;
  const isPaid = paymentStatus === "approved" || invoice.status === "processed";
  const isSubscriptionCancelled =
    preapproval.status === "cancelled" || preapproval.status === "canceled";

  if (!isPaid) {
    const isRejected =
      paymentStatus === "rejected" ||
      invoice.status === "recycling" ||
      invoice.status === "cancelled";
    if (isRejected) {
      await getPrismaClient().professional.updateMany({
        where: {
          id: reference.professionalId,
          expiresAt: { lte: new Date() },
        },
        data: { subscriptionStatus: "EXPIRED" },
      });
    }
    return;
  }

  const paidAtCandidate = new Date(
    invoice.last_modified ?? invoice.date_created ?? Date.now(),
  );
  const paidAt = Number.isNaN(paidAtCandidate.getTime())
    ? new Date()
    : paidAtCandidate;
  const plan = subscriptionPlans[reference.planType];

  try {
    await getPrismaClient().$transaction(async (transaction) => {
      await transaction.subscriptionPayment.create({
        data: {
          mercadoPagoPaymentId: paymentId,
          professionalId: reference.professionalId,
          planType: reference.planType,
          paidAt,
          appliedAt: paidAt,
        },
      });

      const professional = await transaction.professional.findUnique({
        where: { id: reference.professionalId },
        select: { expiresAt: true },
      });
      if (!professional) {
        throw new Error("Subscription payment references an unknown account.");
      }

      const baseTime = Math.max(
        professional.expiresAt.getTime(),
        paidAt.getTime(),
      );
      await transaction.professional.update({
        where: { id: reference.professionalId },
        data: {
          subscriptionStatus: isSubscriptionCancelled ? "CANCELED" : "ACTIVE",
          planType: reference.planType,
          mpSubscriptionId: invoice.preapproval_id,
          expiresAt: new Date(baseTime + plan.durationDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
        },
      });
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2002"
    ) {
      return;
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

async function handlePreferencePayment(paymentId: string): Promise<void> {
  const payment = await new Payment(getClient()).get({ id: paymentId });
  const reference = getPlanReference(payment.external_reference);
  if (!reference) return;

  const paidAtValue = payment.date_approved ?? payment.date_created;
  const paidAt =
    paidAtValue && !Number.isNaN(new Date(paidAtValue).getTime())
      ? new Date(paidAtValue)
      : undefined;
  await syncCheckoutPayment({
    paymentId,
    externalReference: payment.external_reference,
    status: payment.status ?? "unknown",
    statusDetail: payment.status_detail,
    amount: payment.transaction_amount,
    currency: payment.currency_id,
    paymentMethod: payment.payment_method_id,
    paymentType: payment.payment_type_id,
    refundedAmount: payment.transaction_amount_refunded,
    paidAt,
  });

  if (
    (payment.status === "rejected" || payment.status === "cancelled") &&
    payment.status_detail !== "pending_contingency" &&
    payment.status_detail !== "pending_review_manual"
  ) {
    await getPrismaClient().professional.updateMany({
      where: {
        id: reference.professionalId,
        expiresAt: { lte: new Date() },
      },
      data: { subscriptionStatus: "EXPIRED" },
    });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const secret = process.env.MERCADOPAGO_WEBHOOK_SECRET;
  if (!secret) {
    console.error("MERCADOPAGO_WEBHOOK_SECRET is not configured.");
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Webhook is not configured." },
      { status: 503 },
    );
  }

  let payload: WebhookPayload;
  try {
    const body: unknown = await request.json();
    if (!isRecord(body)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Invalid JSON payload." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    payload = {
      type: body.type,
      action: body.action,
      data: isRecord(body.data) ? { id: body.data.id } : undefined,
    };
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Invalid JSON payload." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const payloadId =
    typeof payload.data?.id === "string" ||
    typeof payload.data?.id === "number"
      ? String(payload.data.id)
      : null;
  const dataId = url.searchParams.get("data.id") ?? payloadId;

  try {
    WebhookSignatureValidator.validate({
      xSignature: request.headers.get("x-signature"),
      xRequestId: request.headers.get("x-request-id"),
      dataId,
      secret,
      toleranceSeconds: 300,
    });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Invalid webhook signature." },
      { status: 401 },
    );
  }

  if (!dataId) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Missing resource ID." }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const eventType =
    typeof payload.type === "string"
      ? payload.type
      : typeof payload.action === "string"
        ? payload.action
        : url.searchParams.get("type") ?? url.searchParams.get("topic") ?? "";

  if (
    eventType.includes("subscription_preapproval") ||
    eventType === "preapproval"
  ) {
    await handlePreapproval(dataId);
  } else if (eventType.includes("authorized_payment")) {
    await handleAuthorizedPayment(dataId);
  } else if (eventType === "payment" || eventType.startsWith("payment.")) {
    await handlePreferencePayment(dataId);
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ received: true });
}

      />

        aria-hidden="true"
        className="absolute bottom-[-12rem] right-[-8rem] h-[26rem] w-[26rem] rounded-full bg-[#f0d9e7]/60 blur-3xl"

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\auth\action\page.tsx ---
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

export default async function AuthActionPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const firebaseAuthDomain = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN;
  const mode = params.mode;
  const actionCode = params.oobCode;
  const firstMode = Array.isArray(mode) ? mode[0] : mode;
  const firstActionCode = Array.isArray(actionCode)
    ? actionCode[0]
    : actionCode;

  if (
    !firebaseAuthDomain ||
    !firstMode ||
    !firstActionCode ||
    firstActionCode.length > 10_000
  ) {
    redirect("/login?authAction=invalid");
  }

  const destination = new URL(
    "/__/auth/action",
    `https://${firebaseAuthDomain}`,
  );
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (typeof value === "string") {
      destination.searchParams.set(key, value);
    } else if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const entry of value) destination.searchParams.append(key, entry);
    }
  }
  redirect(destination.toString());
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\layout.tsx ---
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { HelpCircle, LogOut } from "lucide-react";
import { LogoutButton } from "@/components/auth/logout-button";
import { DashboardNavigation } from "@/components/dashboard/dashboard-navigation";
import { WelcomeTour } from "@/components/onboarding/welcome-tour";
import { PlanCards } from "@/components/subscription/plan-cards";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";

export default async function DashboardLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{ children: ReactNode }>) {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });

  const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUnique({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      specialty: true,
      logoUrl: true,
      expiresAt: true,
      subscriptionStatus: true,
      planType: true,
      hasSeenWelcome: true,
      role: true,
    },
  });
  if (!professional) {
    redirect("/login");
  }

  const isExpired = professional.expiresAt <= new Date();
  if (isExpired) {
    if (professional.subscriptionStatus !== "EXPIRED") {
      await getPrismaClient().professional.updateMany({
        where: {
          id: professional.id,
          expiresAt: { lte: new Date() },
          subscriptionStatus: { not: "EXPIRED" },
        },
        data: { subscriptionStatus: "EXPIRED" },
      });
    }
  }

  const expirationLabel = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    dateStyle: "long",
    timeStyle: "short",
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
  }).format(professional.expiresAt);
  const trialEndsAt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    dateStyle: "long",
    timeStyle: "short",
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
  }).format(professional.expiresAt);

  return (
    <>
      <div
        aria-hidden={isExpired}
        className="flex flex-1 flex-col md:flex-row"
        inert={isExpired}
      >
      <aside className="min-w-0 border-b border-[#e9e1ed]/80 bg-white/70 px-4 pb-2 pt-5 backdrop-blur-xl md:sticky md:top-0 md:h-screen md:w-[258px] md:shrink-0 md:border-b-0 md:border-r md:px-5 md:pb-6 md:pt-8">
        <Link
          aria-label="Vitta Beauty — início"
          className="relative block h-[118px] w-[220px] max-w-full overflow-hidden"
          href="/dashboard"
        >
          <Image
            alt="Vitta Beauty"
            className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-[212px] w-[318px] max-w-none -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-[40%]"
            height={408}
            priority
            src="/brand/logo_vitta.png"
            width={612}
          />
        </Link>
        <DashboardNavigation />
        <div className="mt-10 hidden rounded-[20px] border border-[#eee5f2] bg-gradient-to-br from-[#fbf8fc] to-[#f4edf8] p-4 md:block">
          <div className="mb-3 flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-[#9b6bad] shadow-sm">
            <svg aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path d="M12 3v18m9-9H3m15.36-6.36-12.72 12.72m12.72 0L5.64 5.64" />
            </svg>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#49365a]">Um espaço só seu</p>
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-5 text-vitta-muted">
            Sua rotina de beleza e cuidado, organizada com leveza.
          </p>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="flex min-h-[calc(100vh-9.5rem)] min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col md:min-h-screen">
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#e9e1ed]/70 bg-[#f9f7fa]/85 px-3 py-3.5 backdrop-blur-xl sm:gap-4 sm:px-8 md:px-10">
          <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-vitta-muted/75">
                Espaço profissional
              </p>
              <div className="mt-0.5 flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
                <p className="min-w-0 truncate text-[13px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.01em] text-vitta-text">
                  {professional.name}
                </p>
                {professional.role === "ADMIN" && (
                  <Link
                    className="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-vitta/20 bg-white/80 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold leading-none text-vitta transition hover:bg-white"
                    href="/admin"
                  >
                    Admin
                  </Link>
                )}
              </div>
              <p className="truncate text-[11px] font-medium text-vitta-muted">
                {professional.specialty ?? "Profissional da beleza"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
            <span aria-hidden="true" className="hidden h-8 w-px bg-[#e9e1ed] sm:block" />
            <Link
              aria-label="Abrir Minha Conta"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#eedbee] to-[#f7e7ef] text-xs font-semibold text-[#714992] ring-offset-2 transition hover:ring-2 hover:ring-vitta/40 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-vitta"
              href="/dashboard/conta"
            >
              {professional.logoUrl ? (
                <Image
                  alt=""
                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                  height={36}
                  src={professional.logoUrl}
                  unoptimized
                  width={36}
                />
              ) : (
                professional.name.slice(0, 1).toLocaleUpperCase("pt-BR")
              )}
            </Link>
            <LogoutButton className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#e8e0ec] bg-white/80 p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-vitta-text/75 transition hover:border-vitta/30 hover:bg-white hover:text-vitta md:h-auto md:w-auto md:gap-1.5 md:px-3.5 md:py-2">
              <LogOut aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span className="hidden md:inline-block">Sair</span>
            </LogoutButton>
            <Link
              aria-label="Me ajude"
              className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-vitta p-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-vitta/90 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-vitta focus-visible:ring-offset-2 md:h-auto md:w-auto md:gap-1.5 md:px-3.5 md:py-2"
              href="/dashboard/ajuda"
            >
              <HelpCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span className="hidden md:inline-block">Me ajude</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </header>
        <div className="flex-1 px-4 py-7 sm:px-8 sm:py-9 md:px-10">{children}</div>
      </div>
      </div>
      {isExpired && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-black/40 px-3 py-5 backdrop-blur-lg sm:px-6 sm:py-8">
          <section
            aria-labelledby="subscription-gate-title"
            aria-modal="true"
            className="my-auto max-h-full w-full max-w-6xl overflow-y-auto rounded-[28px] border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-[0_24px_80px_rgba(30,18,40,0.35)] sm:p-8 lg:p-10"
            role="dialog"
          >
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-vitta">
              Vitta Beauty · Assinatura
            </p>
            <h1
              className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl"
              id="subscription-gate-title"
            >
              {professional.planType === "FREE"
                ? "Seu período grátis terminou"
                : "Sua assinatura venceu"}
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
              O acesso ao Vitta Beauty foi bloqueado porque seu período de
              utilização terminou em{" "}
              <strong className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                {expirationLabel}
              </strong>
              . Selecione um plano para regularizar o acesso.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-7 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
              <PlanCards horizontal />
            </div>
            <div className="mt-5 text-center">
              <LogoutButton className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-muted underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-vitta">
                Sair da conta
              </LogoutButton>
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
      {!isExpired && !professional.hasSeenWelcome && (
        <WelcomeTour trialEndsAt={trialEndsAt} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { sumMoneyCents } from "@/lib/money";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { formatTime, getCalendarRange } from "@/lib/schedule";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Visão geral",
};

const dashboardItems = [
  {
    href: "/dashboard/clients",
    title: "Clientes",
    eyebrow: "Relacionamento",
    description: "Cadastre contatos e acompanhe o histórico.",
    icon: "clients",
  },
  {
    href: "/dashboard/services",
    title: "Serviços",
    eyebrow: "Seu trabalho",
    description: "Organize seus procedimentos, preços e duração.",
    icon: "services",
  },
  {
    href: "/dashboard/calendar",
    title: "Agenda",
    eyebrow: "Organização",
    description: "Consulte horários e organize atendimentos.",
    icon: "calendar",
  },
  {
    href: "/dashboard/booking-link",
    title: "Meu link de agendamento",
    eyebrow: "Mais praticidade",
    description: "Compartilhe sua página pública pelo WhatsApp.",
    icon: "link",
  },
  {
    href: "/dashboard/finance",
    title: "Financeiro",
    eyebrow: "Controle",
    description: "Acompanhe receitas, despesas e lucro estimado.",
    icon: "finance",
  },
  {
    href: "/dashboard/reports",
    title: "Relatórios",
    eyebrow: "Visão do negócio",
    description: "Veja faturamento, serviços e cancelamentos.",
    icon: "reports",
  },
] as const;

export default async function DashboardPage() {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUnique({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { name: true, timezone: true },
  });
  if (!professional) {
    redirect("/login");
  }
  const firstName = professional.name.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || "profissional";
  const now = new Date();
  const today = getCalendarRange(undefined, "day", professional.timezone);
  const [nextAppointment, pendingAppointments, completedAppointments, income] =
    await Promise.all([
      prisma.appointment.findFirst({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          status: { in: ["PENDING", "CONFIRMED"] },
          startsAt: { gte: now },
        },
        orderBy: { startsAt: "asc" },
        select: {
          startsAt: true,
          client: { select: { name: true } },
          service: { select: { name: true } },
        },
      }),
      prisma.appointment.count({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          status: "PENDING",
          startsAt: { gte: now },
        },
      }),
      prisma.appointment.count({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          status: "COMPLETED",
          startsAt: { gte: today.start, lt: today.end },
        },
      }),
      prisma.transaction.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          type: "INCOME",
          OR: [
            {
              status: "PAID",
              paidAt: { gte: today.start, lt: today.end },
            },
            {
              status: "PENDING",
              dueAt: { gte: today.start, lt: today.end },
            },
            {
              status: "PENDING",
              dueAt: null,
              createdAt: { gte: today.start, lt: today.end },
            },
          ],
        },
        select: { amount: true, status: true },
      }),
    ]);

  const receivedTodayCents = sumMoneyCents(
    income.filter((transaction) => transaction.status === "PAID"),
  );
  const expectedTodayCents = sumMoneyCents(
    income.filter((transaction) => transaction.status === "PENDING"),
  );
  const currency = new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "BRL",
  });
  const widgets = [
    {
      href: "/dashboard/calendar",
      label: "Próximo atendimento",
      value: nextAppointment
        ? formatTime(nextAppointment.startsAt, professional.timezone)
        : "Agenda livre",
      detail: nextAppointment
        ? `${nextAppointment.client.name} · ${nextAppointment.service.name}`
        : "Nenhum atendimento futuro",
      icon: "calendar",
    },
    {
      href: "/dashboard/calendar",
      label: "Aguardando confirmação",
      value: String(pendingAppointments),
      detail:
        pendingAppointments === 1
          ? "1 agendamento pendente"
          : "agendamentos pendentes",
      icon: "pending",
    },
    {
      href: "/dashboard/finance",
      label: "Faturamento de hoje",
      value: currency.format(receivedTodayCents / 100),
      detail: `Previsto: ${currency.format(expectedTodayCents / 100)}`,
      icon: "finance",
    },
    {
      href: "/dashboard/calendar",
      label: "Atendimentos realizados",
      value: String(completedAppointments),
      detail:
        completedAppointments === 1 ? "concluído hoje" : "concluídos hoje",
      icon: "completed",
    },
  ] as const;

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl">
      <div className="relative isolate overflow-hidden rounded-[28px] bg-gradient-to-br from-[#56376f] via-[#7950a5] to-[#a875ae] px-6 py-8 shadow-[0_22px_55px_rgba(86,55,111,0.2)] sm:px-9 sm:py-10 lg:px-11 lg:py-9">
        <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -right-12 -top-24 -z-10 h-72 w-72 rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04]" />
        <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -bottom-36 right-28 -z-10 h-72 w-72 rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04]" />
        <div className="relative grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,0.9fr)_minmax(0,1.1fr)] lg:items-center lg:gap-9">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-white/65">
            Seu espaço, seu momento
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-3 text-[29px] font-semibold leading-[1.12] tracking-[-0.045em] text-white sm:text-[38px]">
            Olá, {firstName}.
            <br className="hidden sm:block" /> Que bom ter você por aqui.
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-3 max-w-lg text-sm leading-6 text-white/75 sm:text-[15px]">
            Cuide da sua rotina profissional com a mesma atenção que você dedica
            a cada cliente.
          </p>
          <Link
            className="mt-6 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-white px-5 py-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#63417c] shadow-sm transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-[#fcf8ff]"
            href="/dashboard/calendar"
          >
            Abrir minha agenda
            <svg aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path d="M5 12h14m-6-6 6 6-6 6" />
            </svg>
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="relative z-10 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:gap-3">
          {widgets.map((widget) => (
            <Link
              className="group min-w-0 rounded-[18px] border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.11] p-3.5 text-white shadow-sm backdrop-blur-md transition hover:border-white/30 hover:bg-white/[0.17] sm:rounded-[20px] sm:p-4"
              href={widget.href}
              key={widget.label}
            >
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase leading-4 tracking-[0.1em] text-white/65 sm:text-[11px]">
                <svg aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-white/75" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="1.7" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path d={widgetIconPaths[widget.icon]} />
                </svg>
                <span className="truncate">{widget.label}</span>
              </span>
              <p className="mt-2 truncate text-lg font-semibold tracking-[-0.03em] sm:text-xl">
                {widget.value}
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 truncate text-[11px] leading-4 text-white/70 sm:text-xs">
                {widget.detail}
              </p>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-2 right-3 -z-10 hidden h-28 w-28 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.07] lg:flex">
          <span className="flex h-[4.5rem] w-[4.5rem] items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/20 bg-white/[0.08] text-[42px] font-light text-white/60">
            V
          </span>
        </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4 mt-9 flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.17em] text-vitta/75">
            Sua rotina
          </p>
          <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.035em] text-vitta-text sm:text-[22px]">
            Acesse seu espaço
          </h2>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {dashboardItems.map((item) => (
          <Link
            className="group rounded-[22px] border border-[#ebe5ef] bg-white/80 p-5 shadow-[0_8px_28px_rgba(48,40,58,0.035)] transition duration-200 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[#d8c8e3] hover:bg-white hover:shadow-card sm:p-5"
            href={item.href}
            key={item.href}
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-[14px] bg-[#f5eff8] text-[#865ba5] transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#efe5f5]">
                <svg aria-hidden="true" className="h-[19px] w-[19px]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="1.7" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path d={homeIconPaths[item.icon]} />
                </svg>
              </span>
              <svg aria-hidden="true" className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 text-[#b8a9c1] transition group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-vitta" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="1.8" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path d="M7 17 17 7M7 7h10v10" />
              </svg>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-vitta-muted/75">
              {item.eyebrow}
            </p>
            <h3 className="mt-1 text-[15px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-vitta-text">
              {item.title}
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-[13px] leading-5 text-vitta-muted">
              {item.description}
            </p>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

const homeIconPaths = {
  clients: "M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H8a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2m6-10a4 4 0 1 0 0-8 4 4 0 0 0 0 8Zm6-7.5a4 4 0 0 1 0 7.75M20 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-3-3.87",

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\agenda\configuracoes\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { WorkingHoursSettings } from "@/components/calendar/working-hours-settings";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Configurações da agenda",
};

export default async function AgendaSettingsPage() {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const savedHours = await getPrismaClient().workingHours.findMany({
    where: { professionalId },
    orderBy: { weekday: "asc" },
  });
  const workingHours = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, weekday) => {
    const saved = savedHours.find((hours) => hours.weekday === weekday);
    return {
      weekday,
      startMinute: saved?.startMinute ?? 9 * 60,
      endMinute: saved?.endMinute ?? 17 * 60,
      interval: saved?.interval ?? 15,
      blockedSlots: saved?.blockedSlots ?? [],
      isActive: saved?.isActive ?? false,
    };
  });

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl">
      <Link
        className="text-sm font-semibold text-vitta hover:underline"
        href="/dashboard/calendar"
      >
        ← Voltar para a agenda
      </Link>
      <div className="mt-4">
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta">Organização</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
          Regras de funcionamento
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Configure o expediente, a frequência dos horários e as pausas
          recorrentes da agenda.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-7">
        <WorkingHoursSettings workingHours={workingHours} />
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Mail } from "lucide-react";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";

function SocialIcon({ network }: { network: "instagram" | "facebook" | "whatsapp" }) {
  if (network === "instagram") {
    return (
      <svg
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="h-5 w-5"
        fill="none"
        stroke="currentColor"
        strokeWidth="1.8"
        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      >
        <rect height="18" rx="5" width="18" x="3" y="3" />
        <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4" />
        <circle cx="17.5" cy="6.5" fill="currentColor" r="1" stroke="none" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (network === "facebook") {
    return (
      <svg
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="h-5 w-5"
        fill="currentColor"
        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      >
        <path d="M13.45 21v-8.2h2.76l.41-3.2h-3.17V7.56c0-.93.26-1.56 1.59-1.56h1.7V3.14C16.44 3.05 15.36 3 14.1 3c-2.63 0-4.43 1.56-4.43 4.43V9.6H6.7v3.2h2.97V21h3.78Z" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  return (
    <svg
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="h-5 w-5"
      fill="currentColor"
      viewBox="0 0 32 32"
    >
      <path d="M16.02 3C8.85 3 3.02 8.77 3.02 15.88c0 2.27.6 4.49 1.75 6.43L3 29l6.9-1.8a13.1 13.1 0 0 0 6.12 1.51h.01c7.16 0 13-5.78 13-12.89 0-3.44-1.35-6.67-3.8-9.1A12.94 12.94 0 0 0 16.02 3Zm0 23.53a10.8 10.8 0 0 1-5.5-1.5l-.4-.23-4.1 1.07 1.1-3.98-.26-.41a10.62 10.62 0 0 1-1.65-5.6c0-5.94 4.86-10.77 10.82-10.77 2.89 0 5.61 1.12 7.65 3.15a10.68 10.68 0 0 1 3.17 7.62c0 5.94-4.86 10.77-10.83 10.77Zm5.94-8.06c-.32-.16-1.9-.94-2.2-1.05-.3-.1-.51-.16-.73.16-.21.32-.83 1.05-1.02 1.26-.19.22-.38.24-.7.08-.32-.16-1.35-.5-2.57-1.58-.95-.84-1.59-1.88-1.78-2.2-.19-.32-.02-.49.14-.65.14-.14.32-.37.48-.55.16-.19.21-.32.32-.53.1-.22.05-.4-.03-.56-.08-.16-.73-1.73-1-2.37-.27-.63-.54-.54-.73-.55h-.62c-.21 0-.56.08-.86.4-.3.32-1.13 1.1-1.13 2.67 0 1.58 1.16 3.1 1.32 3.31.16.21 2.28 3.46 5.52 4.85.77.33 1.37.53 1.84.68.77.24 1.47.21 2.02.13.62-.1 1.9-.78 2.17-1.53.27-.75.27-1.4.19-1.53-.08-.14-.3-.22-.62-.38Z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { CopyBookingLink } from "@/components/booking/copy-booking-link";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { getPublicBookingUrl } from "@/lib/public-url";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Meu link de agendamento",
};

export default async function BookingLinkPage() {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: {
      name: true,
      slug: true,
      services: {
        where: { isActive: true, onlineBookingEnabled: true },
        select: { id: true },
      },
    },
  });
  const url = await getPublicBookingUrl(professional.slug);

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta">Agendamento online</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
        Meu link de agendamento
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
        Compartilhe seu link para que suas clientes consultem os serviços e
        solicitem um horário sem criar uma conta.
      </p>

      <div className="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-8">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">{professional.name}</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
          O link permanece o mesmo e apresenta somente serviços ativos com
          agendamento online habilitado.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-6">
          <CopyBookingLink url={url} />
        </div>

        {professional.services.length === 0 && (
          <p className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-900">
            Você ainda não tem serviços disponíveis para agendamento online.
            Ative essa opção em um serviço para receber solicitações.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\calendar\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { AppointmentControls } from "@/components/calendar/appointment-controls";
import { DeleteCalendarBlockForm } from "@/components/calendar/delete-calendar-block-form";
import { ScheduleForms } from "@/components/calendar/schedule-forms";
import { WhatsAppShortcuts } from "@/components/calendar/whatsapp-shortcuts";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import {
  formatTime,
  getCalendarRange,
  getDateInTimezone,
  parseDateTimeInTimezone,
} from "@/lib/schedule";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { getWhatsAppNumber } from "@/lib/whatsapp";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Agenda",
};

const appointmentStatusLabels = {
  PENDING: "Pendente",
  CONFIRMED: "Confirmado",
  COMPLETED: "Concluído",
  CANCELLED: "Cancelado",
  NO_SHOW: "Não compareceu",
} as const;

function moveCalendarDate(
  dateText: string,
  view: "day" | "week" | "month",
  direction: -1 | 1,
): string {
  const [year, month, day] = dateText.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

  if (view === "month") {
    date.setUTCDate(1);
    date.setUTCMonth(date.getUTCMonth() + direction);
  } else {
    date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + direction * (view === "week" ? 7 : 1));
  }

  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

function calendarHref(date: string, view: string): string {
  return `/dashboard/calendar?date=${date}&view=${view}`;
}

export default async function CalendarPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ date?: string; view?: string }>;
}) {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const params = await searchParams;
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { timezone: true, name: true },
  });
  const range = getCalendarRange(params.date, params.view, professional.timezone);
  const [appointments, blocks, clients, services] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.appointment.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          startsAt: { gte: range.start, lt: range.end },
        },
        orderBy: { startsAt: "asc" },
        include: {
          client: { select: { name: true, whatsapp: true } },
          service: { select: { name: true } },
        },
      }),
      prisma.calendarBlock.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          startsAt: { gte: range.start, lt: range.end },
        },
        orderBy: { startsAt: "asc" },
      }),
      prisma.client.findMany({
        where: { professionalId },
        orderBy: { name: "asc" },
        select: { id: true, name: true, whatsapp: true },
      }),
      prisma.service.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          isActive: true,
        },
        orderBy: { name: "asc" },
        select: { id: true, name: true, durationMinutes: true },
      }),
    ]);

  const firstDay = getDateInTimezone(range.start, professional.timezone);
  const lastDay = getDateInTimezone(
    new Date(range.end.getTime() - 1),
    professional.timezone,
  );
  const days: string[] = [];
  const cursor = new Date(`${firstDay}T00:00:00.000Z`);

  while (cursor.toISOString().slice(0, 10) <= lastDay) {
    days.push(cursor.toISOString().slice(0, 10));
    cursor.setUTCDate(cursor.getUTCDate() + 1);
  }

  const title =
    range.view === "month"
      ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
          timeZone: professional.timezone,
          month: "long",
          year: "numeric",
        }).format(
          parseDateTimeInTimezone(`${range.date}T12:00`, professional.timezone) ??
            new Date(),
        )
      : range.view === "week"
        ? `${new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
            timeZone: professional.timezone,
            day: "2-digit",
            month: "short",
          }).format(range.start)} – ${new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
            timeZone: professional.timezone,
            day: "2-digit",
            month: "short",
          }).format(new Date(range.end.getTime() - 1))}`
        : new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
            timeZone: professional.timezone,
            dateStyle: "full",
          }).format(range.start);

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta">Organização</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
          Agenda
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Consulte compromissos e organize sua rotina.
        </p>
        </div>
        <Link
          className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text transition hover:border-vitta/30 hover:text-vitta"
          href="/dashboard/agenda/configuracoes"
        >
          Configurações de funcionamento
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-7 grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-4 shadow-card sm:p-5">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold capitalize text-vitta-text">
                  {title}
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                  Horário local: {professional.timezone}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Link
                  aria-label="Período anterior"
                  className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text hover:bg-vitta-background"
                  href={calendarHref(
                    moveCalendarDate(range.date, range.view, -1),
                    range.view,
                  )}
                >
                  Anterior
                </Link>
                <Link
                  className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text hover:bg-vitta-background"
                  href="/dashboard/calendar"
                >
                  Hoje
                </Link>
                <Link
                  aria-label="Próximo período"
                  className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text hover:bg-vitta-background"
                  href={calendarHref(
                    moveCalendarDate(range.date, range.view, 1),
                    range.view,
                  )}
                >
                  Próximo
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
            <nav aria-label="Visualização da agenda" className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
              {(["day", "week", "month"] as const).map((view) => (
                <Link
                  aria-current={range.view === view ? "page" : undefined}
                  className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium ${
                    range.view === view
                      ? "bg-vitta text-white"
                      : "border border-vitta-border text-vitta-text hover:bg-vitta-background"
                  }`}
                  href={calendarHref(range.date, view)}
                  key={view}
                >
                  {view === "day"
                    ? "Dia"
                    : view === "week"
                      ? "Semana"
                      : "Mês"}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </nav>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
            {days.map((day) => {
              const dayStart =
                parseDateTimeInTimezone(`${day}T00:00`, professional.timezone) ??
                new Date(`${day}T00:00:00Z`);
              const weekdayLabel = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
                timeZone: professional.timezone,
                weekday: "long",
                day: "numeric",
                month: "long",
              }).format(
                parseDateTimeInTimezone(
                  `${day}T12:00`,
                  professional.timezone,
                ) ?? dayStart,
              );
              const dayAppointments = appointments.filter(
                (appointment) =>
                  getDateInTimezone(
                    appointment.startsAt,
                    professional.timezone,
                  ) === day,
              );
              const dayBlocks = blocks.filter(
                (block) =>
                  getDateInTimezone(block.startsAt, professional.timezone) ===
                  day,
              );
              const entries = [
                ...dayAppointments.map((appointment) => ({
                  type: "appointment" as const,
                  startsAt: appointment.startsAt,
                  appointment,
                })),
                ...dayBlocks.map((block) => ({
                  type: "block" as const,
                  startsAt: block.startsAt,
                  block,
                })),
              ].sort(
                (left, right) =>
                  left.startsAt.getTime() - right.startsAt.getTime(),
              );

              return (
                <section
                  aria-label={weekdayLabel}
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-4 shadow-card sm:p-5"
                  key={day}
                >
                  <h3 className="mb-3 text-sm font-semibold capitalize text-vitta-text">
                    {weekdayLabel}
                  </h3>
                  {entries.length === 0 ? (
                    <p className="rounded-xl bg-vitta-background px-4 py-3 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                      Sem compromissos ou bloqueios.
                    </p>
                  ) : (
                    <ul className="space-y-3">
                      {entries.map((entry) =>
                        entry.type === "appointment" ? (
                          <li
                            className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border p-4"
                            key={entry.appointment.id}
                          >
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
                              <div>
                                <p className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                                  {formatTime(
                                    entry.appointment.startsAt,
                                    professional.timezone,
                                  )}{" "}
                                  –{" "}
                                  {formatTime(
                                    entry.appointment.endsAt,
                                    professional.timezone,
                                  )}
                                </p>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta">
                                  {entry.appointment.client.name}
                                </p>
                              </div>
                              <span className="rounded-full bg-vitta-pink/15 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-text">
                                {appointmentStatusLabels[
                                  entry.appointment.status
                                ]}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                              {entry.appointment.service.name}
                            </p>
                            <a
                              className="mt-1 inline-block text-xs text-vitta hover:underline"
                              href={`https://wa.me/${getWhatsAppNumber(entry.appointment.client.whatsapp)}`}
                              rel="noreferrer"
                              target="_blank"
                            >
                              {entry.appointment.client.whatsapp}
                            </a>
                            <WhatsAppShortcuts
                              clientName={entry.appointment.client.name}
                              endsAt={entry.appointment.endsAt}
                              professionalName={professional.name}
                              serviceName={entry.appointment.service.name}
                              startsAt={entry.appointment.startsAt}
                              status={entry.appointment.status}
                              timeZone={professional.timezone}
                              whatsapp={entry.appointment.client.whatsapp}
                            />
                            {(entry.appointment.status === "PENDING" ||
                              entry.appointment.status === "CONFIRMED") && (
                              <AppointmentControls
                                appointmentId={entry.appointment.id}
                                startsAt={entry.appointment.startsAt}
                                status={entry.appointment.status}
                              />
                            )}
                          </li>
                        ) : (
                          <li
                            className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-vitta-pink bg-vitta-pink/10 p-4"
                            key={entry.block.id}
                          >
                            <p className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                              {formatTime(
                                entry.block.startsAt,
                                professional.timezone,
                              )}{" "}
                              –{" "}
                              {formatTime(
                                entry.block.endsAt,
                                professional.timezone,
                              )}{" "}
                              · Horário bloqueado
                            </p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                              {entry.block.reason ?? "Indisponível"}
                            </p>
                            <DeleteCalendarBlockForm blockId={entry.block.id} />
                          </li>
                        ),
                      )}
                    </ul>
                  )}
                </section>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        <aside className="min-w-0">
          <ScheduleForms
            clients={clients}
            services={services}
          />
        </aside>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\clients\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { ClientForm } from "@/components/clients/client-form";
import { DeleteClientForm } from "@/components/clients/delete-client-form";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/schedule";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { getWhatsAppNumber } from "@/lib/whatsapp";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Clientes",
};

const appointmentStatusLabels = {
  PENDING: "Pendente",
  CONFIRMED: "Confirmado",
  COMPLETED: "Concluído",
  CANCELLED: "Cancelado",
  NO_SHOW: "Não compareceu",
} as const;

export default async function ClientsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ q?: string }>;
}) {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const { q: rawQuery } = await searchParams;
  const query = rawQuery?.trim().slice(0, 100) ?? "";
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const [professional, services, clients] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.professional.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { id: professionalId },
      select: { timezone: true },
    }),
    prisma.service.findMany({
      where: { professionalId },
      orderBy: [{ isActive: "desc" }, { name: "asc" }],
      select: { id: true, name: true, isActive: true },
    }),
    prisma.client.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        ...(query
          ? {
              OR: [
                { name: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" } },
                { whatsapp: { contains: query } },
              ],
            }
          : {}),
      },
      orderBy: { name: "asc" },
      include: {
        preferredService: { select: { id: true, name: true, isActive: true } },
        appointments: {
          orderBy: { startsAt: "desc" },
          take: 10,
          include: {
            service: { select: { name: true } },
          },
        },
        _count: { select: { appointments: true } },
      },
    }),
  ]);
  const selectableServices = services.map((service) => ({
    id: service.id,
    name: service.isActive ? service.name : `${service.name} (inativo)`,
  }));

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl">
      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta">Relacionamento</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
          Clientes
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Cadastre contatos e consulte o histórico de atendimentos.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-7 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(280px,360px)_1fr]">
        <aside className="h-fit rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
          <ClientForm services={services.filter((service) => service.isActive)} />
        </aside>

        <div className="min-w-0">
          <form action="/dashboard/clients" className="mb-4 flex gap-2">
            <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="client-search">
              Buscar clientes
            </label>
            <input
              className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
              defaultValue={query}
              id="client-search"
              name="q"
              placeholder="Buscar por nome ou WhatsApp"
            />
            <button
              className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
              type="submit"
            >
              Buscar
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            {clients.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-vitta-border bg-white p-8 text-center">
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                  {query ? "Nenhum cliente encontrado" : "Sua lista está vazia"}
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                  {query
                    ? "Experimente buscar por outro nome ou telefone."
                    : "Cadastre o primeiro cliente usando o formulário."}
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              clients.map((client) => (
                <article
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6"
                  key={client.id}
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <h2 className="break-words text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                        {client.name}
                      </h2>
                      <a
                        className="mt-1 inline-block text-sm text-vitta hover:underline"
                        href={`https://wa.me/${getWhatsAppNumber(client.whatsapp)}`}
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        target="_blank"
                      >
                        {client.whatsapp}
                      </a>
                    </div>
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-vitta-pink/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-text">
                      {client._count.appointments}{" "}
                      {client._count.appointments === 1
                        ? "atendimento"
                        : "atendimentos"}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <dl className="mt-4 grid gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <div>
                      <dt className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-vitta-muted">
                        Serviço preferido
                      </dt>
                      <dd className="mt-1 text-vitta-text">
                        {client.preferredService?.name ?? "Não informado"}
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  </dl>

                  {client.notes && (
                    <p className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded-xl bg-vitta-background p-3 text-sm text-vitta-text">
                      {client.notes}
                    </p>
                  )}

                  <details className="mt-5 border-t border-vitta-border pt-4">
                    <summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm font-semibold text-vitta">
                      Editar cadastro e ver histórico
                    </summary>
                    <div className="mt-4 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
                      <ClientForm
                        client={client}
                        compact
                        services={selectableServices.filter(
                          (service) =>
                            service.id === client.preferredServiceId ||
                            services.find(
                              (item) => item.id === service.id,
                            )?.isActive,
                        )}
                      />
                      <div>
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                          Histórico recente
                        </h3>
                        {client.appointments.length === 0 ? (
                          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                            Nenhum atendimento registrado.
                          </p>
                        ) : (
                          <ul className="mt-3 divide-y divide-vitta-border">
                            {client.appointments.map((appointment) => (
                              <li
                                className="py-3 text-sm"
                                key={appointment.id}
                              >
                                <p className="font-medium text-vitta-text">
                                  {appointment.service.name}
                                </p>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-vitta-muted">
                                  {formatDateTime(
                                    appointment.startsAt,
                                    professional.timezone,
                                  )}{" "}
                                  ·{" "}
                                  {appointmentStatusLabels[appointment.status]}
                                </p>
                              </li>
                            ))}
                          </ul>
                        )}
                        <div className="mt-4">
                          <DeleteClientForm clientId={client.id} />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </details>
                </article>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\conta\assinatura\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { MercadoPagoConfig, Payment } from "mercadopago";
import { PlanCards } from "@/components/subscription/plan-cards";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { syncCheckoutPayment } from "@/lib/subscription-payments";
import { subscriptionPlans } from "@/lib/subscriptions";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Assinatura",
};

const statusLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  TRIAL: "Período grátis",
  ACTIVE: "Ativa",
  CANCELED: "Cancelada — acesso até o vencimento",
  EXPIRED: "Expirada",
};

type SubscriptionPageProps = {
  searchParams: Promise<{
    collection_id?: string | string[];
    collection_status?: string | string[];
    payment_id?: string | string[];
    status?: string | string[];
  }>;
};

export default async function SubscriptionPage({
  searchParams,
}: SubscriptionPageProps) {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const query = await searchParams;
  const checkoutStatuses = [
    ...(Array.isArray(query.status) ? query.status : [query.status]),
    ...(Array.isArray(query.collection_status)
      ? query.collection_status
      : [query.collection_status]),
  ];
  const checkoutSucceeded = checkoutStatuses.some(
    (status) => status === "success" || status === "approved",
  );
  const paymentIdValue =
    typeof query.payment_id === "string"
      ? query.payment_id
      : typeof query.collection_id === "string"
        ? query.collection_id
        : undefined;
  let paymentNotice: { message: string; tone: "success" | "pending" | "error" } | null =
    null;

  if (checkoutSucceeded) {
    if (!paymentIdValue || !/^\d+$/.test(paymentIdValue)) {
      paymentNotice = {
        message:
          "O Mercado Pago retornou sem o identificador do pagamento. A confirmação será atualizada pelo webhook.",
        tone: "pending",
      };
    } else {
      const accessToken = process.env.MERCADOPAGO_ACCESS_TOKEN;
      if (!accessToken) {
        console.error(
          "MERCADOPAGO_ACCESS_TOKEN is not configured for checkout return validation.",
        );
        paymentNotice = {
          message:
            "Não foi possível consultar o pagamento agora. A confirmação será atualizada pelo webhook.",
          tone: "error",
        };
      } else {
        try {
          const payment = await new Payment(
            new MercadoPagoConfig({ accessToken }),
          ).get({ id: paymentIdValue });

          if (payment.status === "approved") {
            const paidAtValue = payment.date_approved ?? payment.date_created;
            if (!paidAtValue || Number.isNaN(new Date(paidAtValue).getTime())) {
              throw new Error(
                "Approved Mercado Pago payment has no valid creation date.",
              );
            }
            const applied = await syncCheckoutPayment({
              paymentId: paymentIdValue,
              externalReference: payment.external_reference,
              status: payment.status,
              statusDetail: payment.status_detail,
              amount: payment.transaction_amount,
              currency: payment.currency_id,
              paymentMethod: payment.payment_method_id,
              paymentType: payment.payment_type_id,
              refundedAmount: payment.transaction_amount_refunded,
              paidAt: new Date(paidAtValue),
              expectedProfessionalId: professionalId,
            });
            paymentNotice = applied
              ? {
                  message:
                    "Pagamento confirmado. Seu plano e prazo de acesso foram atualizados.",
                  tone: "success",
                }
              : {
                  message:
                    "O pagamento não corresponde a esta conta. O acesso não foi alterado.",
                  tone: "error",
                };
          } else {
            paymentNotice = {
              message:
                "O pagamento ainda está sendo processado. Esta página será atualizada após a confirmação.",
              tone: "pending",
            };
          }
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(
            "[Mercado Pago] Falha ao validar retorno do Checkout Pro:",
            error,
          );
          paymentNotice = {
            message:
              "Não foi possível confirmar o pagamento agora. A confirmação será atualizada pelo webhook.",
            tone: "error",
          };
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUnique({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: {
      subscriptionStatus: true,
      planType: true,
      expiresAt: true,
      mpSubscriptionId: true,
    },
  });
  if (!professional) {
    redirect("/login");
  }
  const currentPlan =
    professional.planType in subscriptionPlans
      ? subscriptionPlans[
          professional.planType as keyof typeof subscriptionPlans
        ].name
      : professional.subscriptionStatus === "TRIAL"
        ? "Período grátis (Trial)"
        : "Gratuito";
  const expiresAt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    dateStyle: "long",
    timeStyle: "short",
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
  }).format(professional.expiresAt);

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl">
      <Link
        className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta transition hover:underline"
        href="/dashboard/conta"
      >
        ← Minha Conta
      </Link>
      <p className="mt-6 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.17em] text-vitta/75">
        Plano e pagamentos
      </p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 text-[28px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.04em] text-vitta-text">
        Assinatura
      </h1>
      {paymentNotice && (
        <p
          className={`mt-4 rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
            paymentNotice.tone === "success"
              ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900"
              : paymentNotice.tone === "error"
                ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800"
                : "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900"
          }`}
          role="status"
        >
          {paymentNotice.message}
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card">
        <p className="text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Status:{" "}
          <strong className="text-vitta-text">
            {statusLabels[professional.subscriptionStatus] ??
              professional.subscriptionStatus}
          </strong>
        </p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Plano vigente:{" "}
          <strong className="text-vitta-text">{currentPlan}</strong>
        </p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Acesso até:{" "}
          <strong className="text-vitta-text">{expiresAt}</strong>
        </p>
        {professional.mpSubscriptionId && (
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
            Assinatura gerenciada pelo Mercado Pago.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      <h2 className="mt-8 text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
        Escolha seu plano
      </h2>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
        O checkout é processado com segurança pelo Mercado Pago.
      </p>
      <div className="mt-4">
        <PlanCards />
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ProfessionalDetailsForm } from "@/components/account/professional-details-form";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Dados Pessoais",
};

export default async function AccountDetailsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ firebaseEmailUpdated?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const emailChanged = Array.isArray(params.firebaseEmailUpdated)
    ? params.firebaseEmailUpdated[0] === "1"
    : params.firebaseEmailUpdated === "1";
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const professional = await getPrismaClient().professional.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: {
      name: true,
      email: true,
      phone: true,
      specialty: true,
      cpf: true,
    },
  });

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl">
      <Link
        className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta transition hover:underline"
        href="/dashboard/conta"
      >
        ← Minha Conta
      </Link>
      <p className="mt-6 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.17em] text-vitta/75">
        Perfil profissional
      </p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 text-[28px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.04em] text-vitta-text">
        Dados Pessoais
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
        Mantenha suas informações e seus dados de contato atualizados.
      </p>

      {emailChanged && <FirebaseEmailChangeSync />}

      <div className="mt-7 rounded-[24px] border border-[#ebe5ef] bg-white/90 p-5 shadow-card sm:p-8">
        <ProfessionalDetailsForm professional={professional} />
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
