--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\finance\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { TransactionForm } from "@/components/finance/transaction-form";
import { TransactionStatusForm } from "@/components/finance/transaction-status-form";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { getReportPeriod } from "@/lib/report-period";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/schedule";
import { sumMoneyCents } from "@/lib/money";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Financeiro",
};

const currency = new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
  style: "currency",
  currency: "BRL",
});

function formatCents(amount: number): string {
  return currency.format(amount / 100);
}

const transactionLabels = {
  INCOME: "Receita",
  EXPENSE: "Despesa",
} as const;

const statusLabels = {
  PENDING: "Pendente",
  PAID: "Pago",
  CANCELLED: "Cancelado",
} as const;

export default async function FinancePage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ from?: string; to?: string; view?: string }>;
}) {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const query = await searchParams;
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { timezone: true },
  });
  const period = getReportPeriod(query.from, query.to, professional.timezone);
  const isRealized = query.view === "realized";
  const now = new Date();
  const forecastFrom = period.from > now ? period.from : now;
  const [
    futureAppointments,
    forecastOtherIncome,
    forecastFixedExpenses,
    realizedIncome,
    realizedServiceExpenses,
    realizedFixedExpenses,
    transactions,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.appointment.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        status: { in: ["PENDING", "CONFIRMED"] },
        startsAt: { gte: forecastFrom, lt: period.to },
      },
      select: {
        priceAtBooking: true,
        service: { select: { materialCost: true } },
      },
    }),
    prisma.transaction.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        type: "INCOME",
        status: "PENDING",
        appointmentId: null,
        OR: [
          { dueAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to } },
          {
            dueAt: null,
            createdAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
          },
        ],
      },
      select: { amount: true },
    }),
    prisma.transaction.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        type: "EXPENSE",
        status: "PENDING",
        appointmentId: null,
        OR: [
          { dueAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to } },
          {
            dueAt: null,
            createdAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
          },
        ],
      },
      select: { amount: true },
    }),
    prisma.transaction.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        type: "INCOME",
        status: "PAID",
        paidAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
      },
      select: { amount: true },
    }),
    prisma.transaction.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        type: "EXPENSE",
        status: "PAID",
        appointmentId: { not: null },
        paidAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
      },
      select: { amount: true },
    }),
    prisma.transaction.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        type: "EXPENSE",
        status: "PAID",
        appointmentId: null,
        paidAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
      },
      select: { amount: true },
    }),
    prisma.transaction.findMany({
      where: {
        professionalId,
        OR: [
          { dueAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to } },
          { paidAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to } },
          {
            AND: [
              { dueAt: null },
              { paidAt: null },
              { createdAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to } },
            ],
          },
        ],
      },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      take: 100,
      include: {
        appointment: {
          select: {
            client: { select: { name: true } },
            service: { select: { name: true } },
          },
        },
      },
    }),
  ]);
  const grossIncomeCents = isRealized
    ? sumMoneyCents(realizedIncome)
    : sumMoneyCents([
        ...futureAppointments.map((appointment) => ({
          amount: appointment.priceAtBooking,
        })),
        ...forecastOtherIncome,
      ]);
  const serviceCostCents = isRealized
    ? sumMoneyCents(realizedServiceExpenses)
    : sumMoneyCents(
        futureAppointments.map((appointment) => ({
          amount: appointment.service.materialCost,
        })),
      );
  const fixedExpenseCents = isRealized
    ? sumMoneyCents(realizedFixedExpenses)
    : sumMoneyCents(forecastFixedExpenses);
  const netProfitCents =
    grossIncomeCents - serviceCostCents - fixedExpenseCents;
  const cards = [
    {
      label: "Faturamento Bruto",
      value: formatCents(grossIncomeCents),
      description: isRealized
        ? "Receitas efetivamente pagas no período"
        : "Agendamentos futuros pendentes ou confirmados e outras receitas previstas",
    },
    {
      label: "Custos de Serviços",
      value: formatCents(serviceCostCents),
      description: isRealized
        ? "Custos de material registrados em atendimentos concluídos"
        : "Materiais estimados para agendamentos pendentes ou confirmados",
    },
    {
      label: "Despesas Fixas",
      value: formatCents(fixedExpenseCents),
      description: isRealized
        ? "Despesas não vinculadas a atendimentos, efetivamente pagas"
        : "Despesas não vinculadas a atendimentos, ainda previstas",
    },
    {
      label: "Lucro Líquido",
      value: formatCents(netProfitCents),
      description: isRealized
        ? "Faturamento recebido menos custos e despesas pagas"
        : "Faturamento previsto menos custos e despesas estimados",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta">Gestão</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
        Financeiro
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
        Registre receitas e despesas e acompanhe valores previstos e recebidos.
      </p>

      <form
        action="/dashboard/finance"
        className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-4 shadow-card"
      >
        <input
          name="view"
          type="hidden"
          value={isRealized ? "realized" : "forecast"}
        />
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          De
          <input
            className="mt-1 block rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5"
            defaultValue={period.fromDate}
            name="from"
            required
            type="date"
          />
        </label>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          Até
          <input
            className="mt-1 block rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5"
            defaultValue={period.toDate}
            name="to"
            required
            type="date"
          />
        </label>
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text hover:bg-vitta-background"
          type="submit"
        >
          Filtrar
        </button>
        <Link
          className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta hover:underline"
          href="/dashboard/reports"
        >
          Ver relatórios
        </Link>
      </form>
      {period.error && (
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-amber-800">{period.error}</p>
      )}

      <nav
        aria-label="Tipo de valores financeiros"
        className="mt-5 inline-flex rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-1 shadow-card"
      >
        {[
          { value: "forecast", label: "Valores Previstos" },
          { value: "realized", label: "Valores Realizados" },
        ].map((tab) => (
          <Link
            aria-current={
              (isRealized ? tab.value === "realized" : tab.value === "forecast")
                ? "page"
                : undefined
            }
            className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition ${
              (isRealized ? tab.value === "realized" : tab.value === "forecast")
                ? "bg-vitta text-white"
                : "text-vitta-muted hover:bg-vitta-background"
            }`}
            href={`/dashboard/finance?from=${encodeURIComponent(period.fromDate)}&to=${encodeURIComponent(period.toDate)}&view=${tab.value}`}
            key={tab.value}
          >
            {tab.label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>

      <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        {cards.map((card) => (
          <article
            className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card"
            key={card.label}
          >
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-muted">{card.label}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text">
              {card.value}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-vitta-muted">
              {card.description}
            </p>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-7 grid items-start gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(280px,360px)_1fr]">
        <aside className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-5 text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Novo lançamento
          </h2>
          <TransactionForm />
        </aside>

        <section>
          <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Lançamentos do período
          </h2>
          {transactions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-vitta-border bg-white p-8 text-center">
              <p className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                Nenhum lançamento neste período
              </p>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                Os atendimentos criados na agenda aparecem como receitas
                previstas. Você também pode registrar despesas e outras receitas.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="space-y-3">
              {transactions.map((transaction) => (
                <li
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-4 shadow-card sm:p-5"
                  key={transaction.id}
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span
                          className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${
                            transaction.type === "INCOME"
                              ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800"
                              : "bg-vitta-pink/15 text-vitta-text"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {transactionLabels[transaction.type]}
                        </span>
                        <span className="rounded-full bg-vitta-background px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-text">
                          {statusLabels[transaction.status]}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-2 break-words font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                        {transaction.description}
                      </p>
                      {(transaction.category || transaction.appointment) && (
                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                          {transaction.category}
                          {transaction.appointment &&
                            ` · ${transaction.appointment.client.name} · ${transaction.appointment.service.name}`}
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                        {transaction.status === "PAID" && transaction.paidAt
                          ? `Pago em ${formatDateTime(transaction.paidAt, professional.timezone)}`
                          : transaction.dueAt
                            ? `Previsto para ${formatDateTime(transaction.dueAt, professional.timezone)}`
                            : `Registrado em ${formatDateTime(transaction.createdAt, professional.timezone)}`}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <p
                      className={`shrink-0 font-semibold ${
                        transaction.type === "INCOME"
                          ? "text-emerald-800"
                          : "text-vitta-text"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {transaction.type === "EXPENSE" ? "−" : "+"}
                      {currency.format(Number(transaction.amount))}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {transaction.status === "PENDING" && (
                    <TransactionStatusForm
                      canCancel={!transaction.appointmentId}
                      transactionId={transaction.id}
                    />
                  )}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
          {transactions.length === 100 && (
            <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
              Exibindo até 100 lançamentos. Use os filtros para restringir o período.
            </p>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\reports\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { getReportPeriod } from "@/lib/report-period";
import { sumMoneyCents } from "@/lib/money";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Relatórios",
};

const currency = new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
  style: "currency",
  currency: "BRL",
});

function formatCents(amount: number): string {
  return currency.format(amount / 100);
}

export default async function ReportsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ from?: string; to?: string }>;
}) {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const query = await searchParams;
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const professional = await prisma.professional.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: professionalId },
    select: { timezone: true },
  });
  const period = getReportPeriod(query.from, query.to, professional.timezone);
  const now = new Date();
  const forecastFrom = period.from > now ? period.from : now;
  const [incomeReceived, incomeExpected, expensesPaid, expensesExpected, appointments] =
    await Promise.all([
      prisma.transaction.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          type: "INCOME",
          status: "PAID",
          paidAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
        },
        select: { amount: true },
      }),
      prisma.transaction.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          type: "INCOME",
          status: "PENDING",
          appointmentId: null,
          OR: [
            { dueAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to } },
            {
              dueAt: null,
              createdAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
            },
          ],
        },
        select: { amount: true },
      }),
      prisma.transaction.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          type: "EXPENSE",
          status: "PAID",
          paidAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
        },
        select: { amount: true },
      }),
      prisma.transaction.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          type: "EXPENSE",
          status: "PENDING",
          appointmentId: null,
          OR: [
            { dueAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to } },
            {
              dueAt: null,
              createdAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
            },
          ],
        },
        select: { amount: true },
      }),
      prisma.appointment.findMany({
        where: {
          professionalId,
          startsAt: { gte: period.from, lt: period.to },
        },
        select: {
          status: true,
          startsAt: true,
          priceAtBooking: true,
          service: {
            select: { id: true, name: true, materialCost: true },
          },
        },
      }),
    ]);

  const completedByService = new Map<
    string,
    { id: string; name: string; count: number }
  >();
  for (const appointment of appointments) {
    if (appointment.status !== "COMPLETED") {
      continue;
    }

    const current = completedByService.get(appointment.service.id);
    completedByService.set(appointment.service.id, {
      id: appointment.service.id,
      name: appointment.service.name,
      count: (current?.count ?? 0) + 1,
    });
  }

  const topServices = [...completedByService.values()]
    .sort((left, right) => right.count - left.count)
    .slice(0, 5);
  const cancelledCount = appointments.filter(
    (appointment) => appointment.status === "CANCELLED",
  ).length;
  const cancellationRate =
    appointments.length > 0
      ? (cancelledCount / appointments.length) * 100
      : 0;
  const incomeReceivedCents = sumMoneyCents(incomeReceived);
  const futureAppointments = appointments.filter(
    (appointment) =>
      (appointment.status === "PENDING" ||
        appointment.status === "CONFIRMED") &&
      appointment.startsAt >= forecastFrom,
  );
  const incomeExpectedCents = sumMoneyCents([
    ...incomeExpected,
    ...futureAppointments.map((appointment) => ({
      amount: appointment.priceAtBooking,
    })),
  ]);
  const expensesPaidCents = sumMoneyCents(expensesPaid);
  const expensesExpectedCents =
    sumMoneyCents(expensesExpected) +
    sumMoneyCents(
      futureAppointments.map((appointment) => ({
        amount: appointment.service.materialCost,
      })),
    );
  const periodLabel = `${period.fromDate} – ${period.toDate}`;
  const cards = [
    {
      label: "Faturamento recebido",
      value: formatCents(incomeReceivedCents),
      description: "Receitas efetivamente pagas no período",
    },
    {
      label: "Receitas previstas",
      value: formatCents(incomeExpectedCents),
      description: "Receitas pendentes com vencimento no período",
    },
    {
      label: "Despesas pagas",
      value: formatCents(expensesPaidCents),
      description: "Despesas efetivamente pagas no período",
    },
    {
      label: "Lucro realizado",
      value: formatCents(incomeReceivedCents - expensesPaidCents),
      description: "Faturamento recebido menos despesas pagas",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta">Acompanhamento</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
        Relatórios
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
        Resumo financeiro e indicadores da sua agenda.
      </p>

      <form
        action="/dashboard/reports"
        className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-4 shadow-card"
      >
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          De
          <input
            className="mt-1 block rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5"
            defaultValue={period.fromDate}
            name="from"
            required
            type="date"
          />
        </label>
        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          Até
          <input
            className="mt-1 block rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5"
            defaultValue={period.toDate}
            name="to"
            required
            type="date"
          />
        </label>
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text hover:bg-vitta-background"
          type="submit"
        >
          Atualizar relatório
        </button>
        <Link
          className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta hover:underline"
          href={`/dashboard/finance?from=${period.fromDate}&to=${period.toDate}`}
        >
          Abrir financeiro
        </Link>
      </form>
      {period.error && (
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-amber-800">{period.error}</p>
      )}
      <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
        Período: {periodLabel}. Faturamento recebido usa a data do pagamento;
        receitas previstas usam a data prevista. O relatório considera
        atendimentos pela data agendada.
      </p>

      <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        {cards.map((card) => (
          <article
            className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card"
            key={card.label}
          >
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-muted">{card.label}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text">
              {card.value}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-vitta-muted">
              {card.description}
            </p>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <article className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-muted">
            Atendimentos agendados
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            {appointments.length}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
            No período selecionado
          </p>
        </article>
        <article className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-muted">
            Cancelamentos
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            {cancellationRate.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
              maximumFractionDigits: 1,
            })}
            %
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
            {cancelledCount} de {appointments.length} atendimentos
          </p>
        </article>
        <article className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-muted">
            Despesas previstas
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            {formatCents(expensesExpectedCents)}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
            Despesas pendentes e materiais de agendamentos futuros pendentes ou confirmados
          </p>
        </article>
      </div>

      <section className="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
          Serviços mais realizados
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Contagem de atendimentos concluídos por serviço.
        </p>
        {topServices.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-vitta-background p-4 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
            Ainda não há atendimentos concluídos neste período.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <ol className="mt-4 divide-y divide-vitta-border">
            {topServices.map((service, index) => (
              <li
                className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 py-3"
                key={service.id}
              >
                <span className="min-w-0 text-sm text-vitta-text">
                  <span className="mr-2 font-semibold text-vitta">
                    {index + 1}.
                  </span>
                  {service.name}
                </span>
                <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-vitta-background px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-text">
                  {service.count}{" "}
                  {service.count === 1 ? "atendimento" : "atendimentos"}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        )}
      </section>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\dashboard\services\page.tsx ---
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { ServiceForm } from "@/components/services/service-form";
import { ToggleServiceActiveForm } from "@/components/services/toggle-service-active-form";
import { getAuthenticatedProfessionalId } from "@/lib/authenticated-professional";
import { getPrismaClient } from "@/lib/prisma";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Serviços",
};

const currency = new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
  style: "currency",
  currency: "BRL",
});

export default async function ServicesPage() {
  const professionalId = await getAuthenticatedProfessionalId({
    allowExpired: true,
  });
  const prisma = getPrismaClient();
  const [services, professional] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.service.findMany({
    where: { professionalId },
    orderBy: [{ isActive: "desc" }, { name: "asc" }],
    }),
    prisma.professional.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { id: professionalId },
      select: { pixKey: true },
    }),
  ]);

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl">
      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta">Catálogo</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl">
          Serviços
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
          Gerencie procedimentos, preços, duração e disponibilidade.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-7 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(280px,360px)_1fr]">
        <aside className="h-fit rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
          <ServiceForm hasPixKey={Boolean(professional.pixKey)} />
        </aside>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {services.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-vitta-border bg-white p-8 text-center">
              <h2 className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                Nenhum serviço cadastrado
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                Cadastre seu primeiro procedimento para organizar a agenda.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            services.map((service) => (
              <article
                className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6"
                key={service.id}
              >
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <h2 className="break-words text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                        {service.name}
                      </h2>
                      <span
                        className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${
                          service.isActive
                            ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800"
                            : "bg-vitta-background text-vitta-muted"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {service.isActive ? "Ativo" : "Inativo"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {service.description && (
                      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
                        {service.description}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="font-semibold text-vitta">
                      {currency.format(Number(service.price))}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                      {service.durationMinutes} minutos
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                      Material: {currency.format(Number(service.materialCost))}
                    </p>
                    {Number(service.depositAmount) > 0 && (
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                        Sinal: {currency.format(Number(service.depositAmount))}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                  Agendamento online:{" "}
                  {service.onlineBookingEnabled ? "permitido" : "desativado"}
                </p>

                <details className="mt-5 border-t border-vitta-border pt-4">
                  <summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm font-semibold text-vitta">
                    Editar serviço
                  </summary>
                  <div className="mt-4 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
                    <ServiceForm
                      service={{
                        id: service.id,
                        name: service.name,
                        description: service.description,
                        price: service.price.toString(),
                        materialCost: service.materialCost.toString(),
                        depositAmount: service.depositAmount?.toString() ?? "0",
                        durationMinutes: service.durationMinutes,
                        onlineBookingEnabled: service.onlineBookingEnabled,
                        isActive: service.isActive,
                      }}
                      hasPixKey={Boolean(professional.pixKey)}
                    />
                    <div>
                      <p className="mb-3 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
                        Desativar um serviço o remove das novas opções de
                        agendamento, sem apagar seu histórico.
                      </p>
                      <ToggleServiceActiveForm
                        active={service.isActive}
                        serviceId={service.id}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </details>
              </article>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\app\termos\page.tsx ---
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Termos de Uso e Política de Privacidade",
  description:
    "Consulte os Termos de Uso e a Política de Privacidade do Vitta Beauty.",
};

type LegalBlock =
  | { type: "paragraph"; text: string }
  | { type: "list"; items: string[] };

type LegalSection = {
  id: string;
  number: string;
  title: string;
  blocks: LegalBlock[];
};

type LegalPart = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  sections: LegalSection[];
};

function stripBoldMarkers(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/\*\*/g, "").trim();
}

function renderEmphasis(text: string) {
  return text.split(/(\*\*[^*]+\*\*)/g).map((part, index) => {
    if (part.startsWith("**") && part.endsWith("**")) {
      return (
        <strong className="font-semibold text-vitta-text" key={index}>
          {part.slice(2, -2)}
        </strong>
      );
    }

    return part;
  });
}

function parseLegalDocument(source: string): {
  effectiveDate: string;
  responsibleDetails: string[];
  parts: LegalPart[];
} {
  const lines = source.split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => line.trim());
  const partLines = lines
    .map((line, index) => ({ line, index }))
    .filter(({ line }) => /^\*\*PARTE [IVX]+ - .+\*\*$/.test(line));

  const dateLine = lines.find((line) => line.startsWith("Data de vigência:"));
  const responsibleHeading = lines.findIndex((line) =>
    line.startsWith("**IDENTIFICAÇÃO DO RESPONSÁVEL"),
  );
  const firstSeparator = lines.findIndex((line) => line.startsWith("# ===="));
  const responsibleDetails =
    responsibleHeading >= 0 && firstSeparator > responsibleHeading
      ? lines
          .slice(responsibleHeading + 1, firstSeparator)
          .filter(Boolean)
      : [];

  const parts = partLines.map(({ line: partLine, index: partIndex }, partNumber) => {
    const endIndex = partLines[partNumber + 1]?.index ?? lines.length;
    const sections: LegalSection[] = [];
    let activeSection: LegalSection | null = null;
    let activeList: string[] = [];

    function flushList() {
      if (activeSection && activeList.length > 0) {
        activeSection.blocks.push({ type: "list", items: activeList });
        activeList = [];
      }
    }

    for (const line of lines.slice(partIndex + 1, endIndex)) {
      if (!line || line.startsWith("# ====")) {
        flushList();
        continue;
      }

      const sectionHeading = /^\*\*(\d+)\.\s+(.+)\*\*$/.exec(line);
      if (sectionHeading) {
        flushList();
        activeSection = {
          id: `${partNumber === 0 ? "terms" : "privacy"}-${sectionHeading[1]}`,
          number: sectionHeading[1],
          title: sectionHeading[2],
          blocks: [],
        };
        sections.push(activeSection);
        continue;
      }

      if (!activeSection) continue;

      const listItem = /^\*\s+(.+)$/.exec(line);
      if (listItem) {
        activeList.push(listItem[1]);
        continue;
      }

      flushList();
      activeSection.blocks.push({ type: "paragraph", text: line });
    }
    flushList();

    return {
      id: partNumber === 0 ? "termos-de-uso" : "politica-de-privacidade",
      title: stripBoldMarkers(partLine),
      sections,
    };
  });

  return {
    effectiveDate: dateLine?.replace("Data de vigência:", "").trim() ?? "",
    responsibleDetails,
    parts,
  };
}

function getLegalDocument() {
  const source = readFileSync(
    join(process.cwd(), "public", "termos-e-politica.txt"),
    "utf8",
  );
  return parseLegalDocument(source);
}

export default async function TermsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ source?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const source = Array.isArray(params.source) ? params.source[0] : params.source;
  const fromAccount = source === "account";
  const backHref = fromAccount ? "/dashboard/conta" : "/cadastro";
  const backLabel = fromAccount ? "Voltar à minha conta" : "Voltar ao cadastro";
  const document = getLegalDocument();

  return (
    <div className="min-h-full bg-[#faf8fc]">
      <header className="relative isolate overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-[#4e3567] via-[#704a91] to-[#9368b3] text-white">
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="absolute -left-24 -top-32 -z-10 h-80 w-80 rounded-full bg-white/10 blur-3xl"
        />
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="absolute -bottom-48 right-[-4rem] -z-10 h-96 w-96 rounded-full bg-[#e7c9e6]/20 blur-3xl"
        />
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-5 pb-12 pt-7 sm:px-8 sm:pb-16 sm:pt-9">
          <Link
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/20 bg-white/10 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white/90 transition hover:bg-white/15"
            href={backHref}
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">←</span>
            {backLabel}
          </Link>

          <div className="mt-10 max-w-3xl sm:mt-14">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-white/20 bg-white/10 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-white/85">
              Vitta Beauty · Documentos legais
            </span>
            <h1 className="mt-5 text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight tracking-[-0.04em] sm:text-5xl">
              Termos de Uso e
              <span className="block text-[#f4dff2]">
                Política de Privacidade

            </h1>

              são tratados para oferecer os serviços do Vitta Beauty.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-white/75">
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10"
              >
                ◷
              </span>
              Vigente desde {document.effectiveDate}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="mx-auto grid w-full max-w-6xl gap-8 px-5 py-8 sm:px-8 sm:py-12 lg:grid-cols-[250px_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:gap-12">
        <aside className="h-fit lg:sticky lg:top-8">
          <nav
            aria-label="Navegação dos documentos"
            className="rounded-[22px] border border-[#ebe5ef] bg-white p-4 shadow-[0_8px_28px_rgba(66,47,78,0.05)] sm:p-5"
          >
            <p className="px-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-vitta-muted">
              Nesta página
            </p>
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-1">
              {document.parts.map((part) => (
                <div key={part.id}>
                  <a
                    className="block rounded-xl px-2 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
                    href={`#${part.id}`}
                  >
                    {part.title.replace(/^PARTE [IVX]+ - /, "")}
                  </a>
                  <div className="ml-2 border-l border-[#eee8f1] pl-2">
                    {part.sections.map((section) => (
                      <a
                        className="block rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-xs leading-5 text-vitta-muted transition hover:bg-vitta-background hover:text-vitta"
                        href={`#${section.id}`}
                        key={section.id}
                      >
                        {section.number}. {section.title}
                      </a>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </nav>

          <section className="mt-4 rounded-[22px] border border-[#ebe5ef] bg-gradient-to-br from-white to-[#f7f1fa] p-5">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-vitta-text">
              Responsável pela plataforma
            </p>
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-xs leading-5 text-vitta-muted">
              {document.responsibleDetails.map((detail) => (
                <p key={detail}>{renderEmphasis(detail)}</p>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        </aside>

        <div className="min-w-0 space-y-10">
          {document.parts.map((part, partIndex) => (
            <section aria-labelledby={`${part.id}-heading`} key={part.id}>
              <div
                className="mb-5 scroll-mt-8 border-b border-[#e9e1ed] pb-4"
                id={part.id}
              >
                <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-vitta/75">
                  Parte {partIndex === 0 ? "01" : "02"}
                </p>
                <h2
                  className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.035em] text-vitta-text sm:text-3xl"
                  id={`${part.id}-heading`}
                >
                  {part.title.replace(/^PARTE [IVX]+ - /, "")}
                </h2>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-4">
                {part.sections.map((section) => (
                  <article
                    aria-labelledby={`${section.id}-heading`}
                    className="scroll-mt-8 rounded-[22px] border border-[#ebe5ef] bg-white p-5 shadow-[0_8px_28px_rgba(66,47,78,0.04)] sm:p-7"
                    id={section.id}
                    key={section.id}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#f4edf8] text-xs font-bold text-vitta">
                        {section.number.padStart(2, "0")}
                      </span>
                      <h3
                        className="pt-1 text-base font-semibold leading-6 text-vitta-text sm:text-lg"
                        id={`${section.id}-heading`}
                      >
                        {section.title}
                      </h3>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-4 space-y-3 pl-0 text-sm leading-7 text-[#62596a] sm:pl-12">
                      {section.blocks.map((block, blockIndex) =>
                        block.type === "list" ? (
                          <ul
                            className="space-y-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-[#faf8fc] px-4 py-4 sm:px-5"
                            key={`${section.id}-list-${blockIndex}`}
                          >
                            {block.items.map((item, itemIndex) => (
                              <li
                                className="flex gap-3"
                                key={`${section.id}-item-${itemIndex}`}
                              >
                                <span
                                  aria-hidden="true"
                                  className="mt-3 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[#a47bb9]"
                                />
                                <span>{renderEmphasis(item)}</span>
                              </li>
                            ))}
                          </ul>
                        ) : (
                          <p key={`${section.id}-paragraph-${blockIndex}`}>
                            {renderEmphasis(block.text)}
                          </p>
                        ),
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          ))}

          <div className="rounded-[22px] border border-[#e8dced] bg-[#f4edf8] p-5 text-center sm:p-7">
            <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-text">
              Ao criar uma conta, você confirma que leu e compreendeu estes
              documentos.
            </p>
            <Link
              className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-vitta px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-vitta/90"
              href={backHref}
            >
              {backLabel}
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\account\delete-account-form.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useTransition, type FormEvent } from "react";
import {
  EmailAuthProvider,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  reauthenticateWithCredential,
  reauthenticateWithPopup,
  signOut,
  type User,
} from "firebase/auth";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { deleteProfessionalAccountAction } from "@/app/actions/account-deletion";
import { PasswordInput } from "@/components/forms/password-input";
import { auth, googleProvider } from "@/lib/firebase";

type DeleteAccountFormProps = {
  expiresAt: Date;
  hasActiveAccess: boolean;
  planType: string;
  subscriptionStatus: string;
};

function getAccessExpiryMessage(status: string, expiresAt: Date): string {
  const date = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    dateStyle: "long",
    timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
  }).format(expiresAt);

  if (status === "TRIAL") {
    return `Seu período de teste grátis termina em ${date}.`;
  }
  if (status === "EXPIRED") {
    return `Seu período de acesso expirou em ${date}.`;
  }
  if (status === "CANCELED" || status === "CANCELLED") {
    return `Seu acesso atual permanece disponível até ${date}.`;
  }
  return `Sua assinatura dá acesso à plataforma até ${date}.`;
}

function getPlanLabel(planType: string): string {
  const planLabels: Record<string, string> = {
    FREE: "Gratuito",
    MONTHLY: "Mensal",
    SEMIANNUAL: "Semestral",
    ANNUAL: "Anual",
  };
  return planLabels[planType] ?? planType;
}

export function DeleteAccountForm({
  expiresAt,
  hasActiveAccess,
  planType,
  subscriptionStatus,
}: DeleteAccountFormProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [cpf, setCpf] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [step, setStep] = useState<"explanation" | "confirmation">(
    "explanation",
  );
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const hasGoogleProvider = user?.providerData.some(
    (provider) => provider.providerId === "google.com",
  );
  const hasPasswordProvider = user?.providerData.some(
    (provider) => provider.providerId === "password",
  );
  const canSubmit = Boolean(
    user &&
      email.trim() &&
      cpf.trim() &&
      (hasGoogleProvider || (hasPasswordProvider && password)),
  );
  const accessExpiryMessage = getAccessExpiryMessage(
    subscriptionStatus,
    expiresAt,
  );

  useEffect(() => onAuthStateChanged(auth, setUser), []);

  function closeDialog() {
    if (pending) return;
    setDialogOpen(false);
    setStep("explanation");
    setError("");
    setPassword("");
  }

  function continueToConfirmation() {
    setError("");
    setStep("confirmation");
  }

  function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const currentUser = user;
        if (!currentUser) {
          setError("Sua sessão expirou. Entre novamente para continuar.");
          return;
        }

        if (hasGoogleProvider) {
          await reauthenticateWithPopup(currentUser, googleProvider);
        } else if (hasPasswordProvider && currentUser.email) {
          await reauthenticateWithCredential(
            currentUser,
            EmailAuthProvider.credential(currentUser.email, password),
          );
        } else {
          setError(
            "Não foi possível confirmar sua identidade com o método de acesso desta conta.",
          );
          return;
        }

        const result = await deleteProfessionalAccountAction(
          email,
          cpf,
          await currentUser.getIdToken(true),
        );
        if (result.error) {
          if (result.cleanupPending) {
            await signOut(auth);
            router.replace("/login?accountDeletionPending=1");
            router.refresh();
            return;
          }
          setError(result.error);
          return;
        }

        await signOut(auth).catch((signOutError: unknown) => {
          console.error(
            "Could not clear Firebase client state after account deletion.",
            signOutError,
          );
        });
        router.replace("/login?accountDeleted=1");
        router.refresh();
      } catch (deleteError) {
        const code =
          typeof deleteError === "object" &&
          deleteError !== null &&
          "code" in deleteError &&
          typeof deleteError.code === "string"
            ? deleteError.code
            : "";
        setError(
          code === "auth/wrong-password" ||
            code === "auth/invalid-credential"
            ? "A senha informada está incorreta."
            : code === "auth/popup-closed-by-user"
              ? "A confirmação pela conta Google foi cancelada."
              : code === "auth/requires-recent-login"
                ? "Confirme sua identidade novamente para excluir a conta."
                : "Não foi possível confirmar sua identidade ou excluir a conta.",
        );
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="mt-8 rounded-[24px] border border-rose-200 bg-white/90 p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
        <button
          className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-rose-300 bg-rose-50 px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-rose-800 transition hover:bg-rose-100 sm:w-auto"
          onClick={() => {
            setStep("explanation");
            setDialogOpen(true);
          }}
          type="button"
        >
          Excluir minha conta
        </button>
      </div>

      {dialogOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[120] flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-[#24182d]/55 px-4 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
          onMouseDown={(event) => {
            if (event.target === event.currentTarget) closeDialog();
          }}
        >
          <section
            aria-labelledby="delete-account-dialog-title"
            aria-modal="true"
            className="my-auto w-full max-w-xl rounded-[28px] border border-white/80 bg-white p-5 shadow-[0_28px_90px_rgba(27,17,34,0.28)] sm:p-8"
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === "Escape") closeDialog();
            }}
            role="dialog"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.17em] text-rose-700/80">
                  Ação permanente
                </p>
                <h2
                  className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.03em] text-vitta-text sm:text-2xl"
                  id="delete-account-dialog-title"
                >
                  {step === "explanation"
                    ? "Antes de excluir sua conta"
                    : "Confirme sua identidade"}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button
                aria-label="Fechar"
                className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-vitta-muted transition hover:bg-vitta-background hover:text-vitta-text"
                disabled={pending}
                onClick={closeDialog}
                type="button"
              >
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-xl leading-none">
                  ×
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {step === "explanation" ? (
              <>
                <div className="mt-5 space-y-4 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
                  <p>
                    A exclusão removerá permanentemente seu perfil e todos os
                    dados vinculados, incluindo clientes, serviços, agenda,
                    atendimentos, configurações e informações da plataforma.
                    Uma assinatura ativa será cancelada antes da exclusão.
                  </p>
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background/70 p-4">
                    <p className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                      {accessExpiryMessage}
                    </p>
                    {hasActiveAccess && (
                      <p className="mt-1.5">
                        Recomendamos continuar usando a plataforma até essa
                        data, se quiser aproveitar o período já disponível.
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <p className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-amber-950">
                    Após a exclusão, o acesso será encerrado imediatamente.
                    Valores já pagos não serão reembolsados nem convertidos em
                    crédito.
                  </p>
                  {planType && (
                    <p className="text-xs">
                      Plano atual:{" "}
                      <span className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                        {getPlanLabel(planType)}
                      </span>
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
                  <button
                    className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
                    onClick={closeDialog}
                    type="button"
                  >
                    Cancelar
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className="rounded-2xl bg-rose-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-rose-800"
                    onClick={continueToConfirmation}
                    type="button"
                  >
                    Concordo e continuar
                  </button>
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <form className="mt-5 space-y-4" onSubmit={submit}>
                <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
                  Para prosseguir, informe o e-mail e o CPF cadastrados e
                  confirme sua identidade.
                </p>

                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
                  E-mail cadastrado
                  <input
                    autoComplete="email"
                    className="mt-2 w-full rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#fcfbfd] px-4 py-3.5 text-vitta-text outline-none transition focus:border-vitta/50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/10"
                    maxLength={254}
                    onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.currentTarget.value)}
                    required
                    type="email"
                    value={email}
                  />
                </label>

                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
                  CPF cadastrado
                  <input
                    autoComplete="off"
                    className="mt-2 w-full rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#fcfbfd] px-4 py-3.5 text-vitta-text outline-none transition focus:border-vitta/50 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/10"
                    inputMode="numeric"
                    maxLength={14}
                    onChange={(event) => setCpf(event.currentTarget.value)}
                    required
                    value={cpf}
                  />
                </label>

                {user && hasGoogleProvider ? (
                  <p className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background/70 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
                    Esta conta usa o Google para entrar. Ao continuar, será
                    solicitada uma nova confirmação da sua conta Google.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <PasswordInput
                    autoComplete="current-password"
                    id="delete-account-password"
                    label="Senha atual"
                    maxLength={72}
                    name="deleteAccountPassword"
                    onChange={setPassword}
                    value={password}
                  />
                )}

                {error && (
                  <p
                    className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-rose-800"
                    role="alert"
                  >
                    {error}
                  </p>
                )}

                <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 pt-2 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
                  <button
                    className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
                    disabled={pending}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setError("");
                      setStep("explanation");
                    }}
                    type="button"
                  >
                    Voltar
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className="rounded-2xl bg-rose-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-rose-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                    disabled={pending || !canSubmit}
                    type="submit"
                  >
                    {pending ? "Excluindo conta..." : "Confirmar exclusão"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\account\firebase-email-change-sync.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { onAuthStateChanged, reload } from "firebase/auth";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { syncProfessionalEmailFromFirebaseAction } from "@/app/actions/account";
import { auth } from "@/lib/firebase";

export function FirebaseEmailChangeSync() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const started = useRef(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState(
    "Confirmando a atualização do e-mail da sua conta...",
  );
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
      if (started.current) return;
      started.current = true;

      if (!user) {
        if (active) {
          setFailed(true);
          setMessage(
            "O Firebase confirmou a alteração, mas não encontramos sua sessão. Entre novamente para sincronizar o e-mail.",
          );
        }
        return;
      }

      try {
        await reload(user);
        const result = await syncProfessionalEmailFromFirebaseAction(
          await user.getIdToken(true),
        );
        if (result.error) {
          if (active) {
            setFailed(true);
            setMessage(result.error);
          }
          return;
        }
        if (active) {
          router.replace("/dashboard/conta/dados");
          router.refresh();
        }
      } catch {
        if (active) {
          setFailed(true);
          setMessage(
            "Não foi possível sincronizar o e-mail. Atualize a página ou entre novamente.",
          );
        }
      }
    });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [router]);

  return (
    <p
      className={`mt-5 rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm ${
        failed
          ? "border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-800"
          : "border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background text-vitta-muted"
      }`}
      role={failed ? "alert" : "status"}
    >
      {message}
    </p>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\account\professional-details-form.tsx ---
"use client";

import {
  useEffect,
  useState,
  useTransition,
  type FormEvent,
} from "react";
import {
  EmailAuthProvider,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  reauthenticateWithCredential,
  verifyBeforeUpdateEmail,
  type User,
} from "firebase/auth";
import { Pencil, X } from "lucide-react";
import {
  checkProfessionalEmailAction,
  updateProfessionalDetailsAction,
} from "@/app/actions/account";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { FormFeedback } from "@/components/forms/form-feedback";
import { getFirebaseActionCodeSettings } from "@/lib/firebase-action-settings";
import { PasswordInput } from "@/components/forms/password-input";
import { auth } from "@/lib/firebase";
import {
  formatAccountPhone,
  formatCpf,
} from "@/lib/professional-validation";

type ProfessionalDetails = {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string | null;
  specialty: string | null;
  cpf: string | null;
};

const initialState: FormState = {};
const inputClass =
  "mt-2 w-full rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#fcfbfd] px-4 py-3.5 text-vitta-text outline-none transition";

export function ProfessionalDetailsForm({
  professional,
}: {
  professional: ProfessionalDetails;
}) {
  const [state, setState] = useState(initialState);
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const [emailPending, startEmailTransition] = useTransition();
  const [firebaseUser, setFirebaseUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(professional.email);
  const [editingEmail, setEditingEmail] = useState(false);
  const [emailChangeRequested, setEmailChangeRequested] = useState(false);
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState("");
  const emailChanged = email.trim().toLowerCase() !== professional.email;
  const googleAccount = firebaseUser?.providerData.some(
    (provider) => provider.providerId === "google.com",
  );

  useEffect(
    () => onAuthStateChanged(auth, setFirebaseUser),
    [],
  );

  function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    setState(initialState);

    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const result = await updateProfessionalDetailsAction(formData);
        if (result.error) {
          setState(result);
          return;
        }

        setState(result);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Could not save professional profile details.", error);
        const code =
          typeof error === "object" &&
          error !== null &&
          "code" in error &&
          typeof error.code === "string"
            ? error.code
            : "";
        setState({
          error:
            code === "auth/wrong-password" ||
            code === "auth/invalid-credential"
              ? "A senha atual está incorreta."
              : code === "auth/requires-recent-login"
                ? "A sessão expirou. Entre novamente e tente alterar o e-mail."
                : code === "auth/email-already-in-use"
                  ? "Este e-mail já está sendo usado por outra conta Firebase."
                  : "Não foi possível atualizar os dados. Tente novamente.",
        });
      }
    });
  }

  function requestEmailChange() {
    setState(initialState);
    startEmailTransition(async () => {
      try {
        if (googleAccount) {
          setState({
            error: "Contas acessadas pelo Google não podem alterar o e-mail.",
          });
          return;
        }

        const user = firebaseUser;
        const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!user?.email || !user.providerData.some(
          (provider) => provider.providerId === "password",
        )) {
          setState({
            error: "Esta conta não possui acesso por e-mail e senha.",
          });
          return;
        }
        if (!emailChanged) {
          setState({ error: "Informe um e-mail diferente do atual." });
          return;
        }

        const availability = await checkProfessionalEmailAction(normalizedEmail);
        if (availability.error) {
          setState(availability);
          return;
        }
        if (!currentPassword) {
          setState({ error: "Informe sua senha atual para confirmar a alteração." });
          return;
        }

        await reauthenticateWithCredential(
          user,
          EmailAuthProvider.credential(user.email, currentPassword),
        );
        await verifyBeforeUpdateEmail(
          user,
          normalizedEmail,
          getFirebaseActionCodeSettings(
            "/dashboard/conta/dados?firebaseEmailUpdated=1",
          ),
        );
        setEmailChangeRequested(true);
        setEditingEmail(false);
        setCurrentPassword("");
        setState({
          success:
            `Enviamos um link de confirmação para ${normalizedEmail}. Abra a caixa de entrada desse novo endereço e clique no link. A troca só será concluída após essa confirmação; ao voltar ao Vitta Beauty, seu perfil será atualizado.`,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Could not request Firebase email change.", error);
        const code =
          typeof error === "object" &&
          error !== null &&
          "code" in error &&
          typeof error.code === "string"
            ? error.code
            : "";
        setState({
          error:
            code === "auth/wrong-password" ||
            code === "auth/invalid-credential"
              ? "A senha atual está incorreta."
              : code === "auth/requires-recent-login"
                ? "A sessão expirou. Entre novamente e tente alterar o e-mail."
                : code === "auth/email-already-in-use"
                  ? "Este e-mail já está sendo usado por outra conta Firebase."
                  : "Não foi possível enviar a confirmação do novo e-mail.",
        });
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
        Nome profissional
        <input
          autoComplete="organization"
          className={inputClass}
          defaultValue={professional.name}
          maxLength={80}
          name="name"
          placeholder="Ex.: Ana HairStudio ou Ana Santos"
          required
        />
        <span className="mt-1.5 block text-xs font-normal text-vitta-muted">
          Informe pelo menos duas palavras.
        </span>
      </label>

      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <label
            className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text"
            htmlFor="professional-email"
          >
            E-mail
          </label>
          {firebaseUser && !googleAccount && !emailChangeRequested && (
            <button
              aria-label={editingEmail ? "Cancelar alteração de e-mail" : "Editar e-mail"}
              className="inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-vitta transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
              onClick={() => {
                setState(initialState);
                setEmail(editingEmail ? professional.email : email);
                setEditingEmail((current) => !current);
                setCurrentPassword("");
              }}
              type="button"
            >
              {editingEmail ? (
                <X aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
              ) : (
                <Pencil aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />
              )}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <input
          autoComplete="email"
          className={`${inputClass} ${!editingEmail || googleAccount || emailChangeRequested ? "cursor-not-allowed bg-[#f3f0f5] text-vitta-muted" : ""}`}
          id="professional-email"
          maxLength={254}
          onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.currentTarget.value)}
          readOnly={!editingEmail || !!googleAccount || emailChangeRequested}
          type="email"
          value={email}
        />
        {googleAccount && (
          <span className="mt-1.5 block text-xs text-vitta-muted">
            O e-mail é gerenciado pelo Google e não pode ser alterado aqui.
          </span>
        )}
        {editingEmail && !googleAccount && (
          <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background/50 p-4">
            <PasswordInput
              autoComplete="current-password"
              id="email-change-current-password"
              label="Senha atual para confirmar"
              maxLength={72}
              name="emailChangeCurrentPassword"
              onChange={setCurrentPassword}
              value={currentPassword}
            />
            <button
              className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60 sm:w-auto"
              disabled={emailPending || !emailChanged || !currentPassword}
              onClick={requestEmailChange}
              type="button"
            >
              {emailPending
                ? "Enviando link..."
                : "Enviar link de confirmação"}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        {emailChangeRequested && (
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-vitta-muted">
            Aguardando o clique no link enviado para o novo e-mail. A alteração
            não acontece automaticamente ao solicitar o link.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
        Telefone / WhatsApp
        <input
          autoComplete="tel"
          className={inputClass}
          defaultValue={formatAccountPhone(professional.phone ?? "")}
          inputMode="tel"
          maxLength={16}
          name="phone"
          onChange={(event) => {
            event.currentTarget.value = formatAccountPhone(
              event.currentTarget.value,
            );
          }}
          placeholder="(11) 99999-9999"
          required
          type="tel"
        />
      </label>

      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
        Especialidade
        <input
          autoComplete="organization-title"
          className={inputClass}
          defaultValue={professional.specialty ?? ""}
          maxLength={80}
          name="specialty"
          placeholder="Ex.: Cabeleireira, Manicure"
          required
        />
      </label>

      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
        CPF
        <input
          aria-describedby="cpf-readonly-help"
          className="mt-2 w-full cursor-not-allowed rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#f3f0f5] px-4 py-3.5 text-vitta-muted outline-none"
          readOnly
          value={professional.cpf ? formatCpf(professional.cpf) : "Não informado"}
        />
        <span
          className="mt-1.5 block text-xs font-normal text-vitta-muted"
          id="cpf-readonly-help"
        >
          O CPF é fixo e não pode ser alterado por aqui.
        </span>
      </label>

      <FormFeedback state={state} />
      <button
        className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-white shadow-[0_8px_18px_rgba(121,80,165,0.2)] transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60 sm:w-auto sm:min-w-52"
        disabled={pending}
        type="submit"
      >
        {pending ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar Alterações"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\account\profile-image-upload.tsx ---
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { updateProfileImageAction } from "@/app/actions/account";

const maximumImageSize = 50 * 1024;
const imageDimension = 256;

function loadImage(file: File): Promise<HTMLImageElement> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const image = new window.Image();
    const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);

    image.onload = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
      resolve(image);
    };
    image.onerror = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
      reject(new Error("Não foi possível abrir esta imagem."));
    };
    image.src = objectUrl;
  });
}

function canvasToWebp(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, quality: number) {
  return new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
    canvas.toBlob(
      (blob) => {
        if (!blob) {
          reject(new Error("Não foi possível comprimir a imagem."));
          return;
        }
        resolve(blob);
      },
      "image/webp",
      quality,
    );
  });
}

function blobToDataUrl(blob: Blob): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      if (typeof reader.result !== "string") {
        reject(new Error("Não foi possível preparar a imagem."));
        return;
      }
      resolve(reader.result);
    };
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Não foi possível ler a imagem."));
    reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });
}

async function compressImage(file: File): Promise<string> {
  if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
    throw new Error("Selecione um arquivo de imagem.");
  }

  const image = await loadImage(file);
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  canvas.width = imageDimension;
  canvas.height = imageDimension;

  const scale = Math.max(
    imageDimension / image.naturalWidth,
    imageDimension / image.naturalHeight,
  );
  const width = image.naturalWidth * scale;
  const height = image.naturalHeight * scale;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("Seu navegador não conseguiu processar a imagem.");
  }

  context.drawImage(
    image,
    (imageDimension - width) / 2,
    (imageDimension - height) / 2,
    width,
    height,
  );

  for (const quality of [0.7, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4]) {
    const blob = await canvasToWebp(canvas, quality);
    const dataUrl = await blobToDataUrl(blob);
    if (new TextEncoder().encode(dataUrl).byteLength <= maximumImageSize) {
      return dataUrl;
    }
  }

  throw new Error(
    "A imagem ainda ficou maior que 50 KB após a compressão. Escolha outra imagem.",
  );
}

export function ProfileImageUpload({
  logoUrl,
  name,
}: {
  logoUrl: string | null;
  name: string;
}) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const initials = name
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((part) => part[0]?.toLocaleUpperCase("pt-BR"))
    .join("");

  async function handleFileChange(
    event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
  ) {
    const file = event.currentTarget.files?.[0];
    event.currentTarget.value = "";
    if (!file) return;

    setPending(true);
    setError(null);
    setSuccess(null);
    try {
      const dataUrl = await compressImage(file);
      const result = await updateProfileImageAction(dataUrl);
      if (result.error) {
        setError(result.error);
        return;
      }
      setSuccess(result.success ?? "Foto de perfil atualizada.");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (uploadError) {
      setError(
        uploadError instanceof Error
          ? uploadError.message
          : "Não foi possível atualizar a foto de perfil.",
      );
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
      <input
        accept="image/*"
        className="sr-only"
        onChange={handleFileChange}
        ref={inputRef}
        type="file"
      />
      <button
        aria-label={
          pending
            ? "Processando foto de perfil"
            : `Alterar foto de perfil de ${name}`
        }
        className="group relative flex h-24 w-24 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border-4 border-white bg-gradient-to-br from-[#eedbee] to-[#f7e7ef] text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-[#714992] shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(66,47,78,0.12)] disabled:cursor-wait"
        disabled={pending}
        onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}
        type="button"
      >
        {logoUrl ? (
          <Image
            alt={`Foto de perfil de ${name}`}
            className="object-cover"
            height={96}
            priority
            src={logoUrl}
            unoptimized
            width={96}
          />
        ) : (
          <span aria-hidden="true">{initials || "VB"}</span>
        )}
        <span className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/45 text-xs font-semibold text-white opacity-0 transition group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-visible:opacity-100">
          {pending ? "Salvando..." : "Alterar foto"}
        </span>
        {pending && (
          <span className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/55 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
            Enviando...
          </span>
        )}
      </button>
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
        Toque na foto para enviar uma imagem
      </p>
      {error && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-red-700" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      {success && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-emerald-700" role="status">
          {success}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
