--- ARQUIVO: src\components\account\security-form.tsx ---
"use client";

import {
  useEffect,
  useState,
  useTransition,
  type FormEvent,
} from "react";
import {
  EmailAuthProvider,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  reauthenticateWithCredential,
  updatePassword as updateFirebasePassword,
  type User,
} from "firebase/auth";
import { recordFirebasePasswordChangeAction } from "@/app/actions/account";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { FormFeedback } from "@/components/forms/form-feedback";
import { PasswordInput } from "@/components/forms/password-input";
import { getPasswordRequirements } from "@/lib/professional-validation";
import { auth } from "@/lib/firebase";

const initialState: FormState = {};

export function SecurityForm() {
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [unlocked, setUnlocked] = useState(false);
  const [firebaseUser, setFirebaseUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [verifyState, setVerifyState] = useState<FormState>(initialState);
  const [updateState, setUpdateState] = useState<FormState>(initialState);
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const verified = unlocked;
  const requirements = getPasswordRequirements(newPassword);
  const passwordsMatch =
    confirmPassword.length > 0 && newPassword === confirmPassword;
  const passwordProvider = firebaseUser?.providerData.some(
    (provider) => provider.providerId === "password",
  );
  const googleAccount = firebaseUser?.providerData.some(
    (provider) => provider.providerId === "google.com",
  );
  useEffect(
    () => onAuthStateChanged(auth, setFirebaseUser),
    [],
  );

  function verifyCurrentPassword(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setUpdateState(initialState);

    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const user = firebaseUser;
        if (!user?.email || !passwordProvider) {
          setVerifyState({
            error: "Esta conta não possui uma senha de acesso para revalidar.",
          });
          return;
        }
        await reauthenticateWithCredential(
          user,
          EmailAuthProvider.credential(user.email, currentPassword),
        );
        setVerifyState({ success: "Senha atual confirmada." });
        setUnlocked(true);
      } catch (error) {
        const code =
          typeof error === "object" &&
          error !== null &&
          "code" in error &&
          typeof error.code === "string"
            ? error.code
            : "";
        setVerifyState({
          error:
            code === "auth/wrong-password" ||
            code === "auth/invalid-credential"
              ? "A senha atual está incorreta."
              : code === "auth/requires-recent-login"
                ? "Entre novamente e tente alterar sua senha."
                : "Não foi possível confirmar a senha atual.",
        });
      }
    });
  }

  function updatePassword(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const user = firebaseUser;
        if (!user || !unlocked) {
          setUpdateState({
            error: "Confirme sua senha atual antes de continuar.",
          });
          return;
        }
        if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
          setUpdateState({
            error: "A confirmação não corresponde à nova senha.",
          });
          return;
        }
        if (newPassword === currentPassword) {
          setUpdateState({
            error: "Escolha uma senha diferente da senha atual.",
          });
          return;
        }
        if (
          getPasswordRequirements(newPassword).some(
            (requirement) => !requirement.met,
          )
        ) {
          setUpdateState({
            error: "A nova senha não cumpre todos os requisitos de segurança.",
          });
          return;
        }

        await updateFirebasePassword(user, newPassword);
        const logResult = await recordFirebasePasswordChangeAction(
          await user.getIdToken(true),
        );
        if (logResult.error) {
          setUpdateState({
            error:
              "Sua senha foi alterada, mas não conseguimos confirmar a operação. Entre novamente se tiver problemas de acesso.",
          });
          return;
        }
        setUpdateState({ success: "Senha atualizada com segurança." });
        setUnlocked(false);
        setVerifyState(initialState);
        setCurrentPassword("");
        setNewPassword("");
        setConfirmPassword("");
      } catch (error) {
        const code =
          typeof error === "object" &&
          error !== null &&
          "code" in error &&
          typeof error.code === "string"
            ? error.code
            : "";
        setUpdateState({
          error:
            code === "auth/requires-recent-login"
              ? "Entre novamente e repita a confirmação da senha atual."
              : code === "auth/weak-password"
                ? "A nova senha não atende aos requisitos de segurança."
                : "Não foi possível atualizar a senha. Tente novamente.",
        });
      }
    });
  }

  if (passwordProvider === undefined) {
    return (
      <p className="text-sm text-vitta-muted">Carregando os dados de acesso…</p>
    );
  }

  if (googleAccount) {
    return (
      <p className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background/70 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
        Contas acessadas pelo Google não podem alterar a senha por aqui.
      </p>
    );
  }

  if (!passwordProvider) {
    return (
      <p className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background/70 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
        Esta conta não possui uma senha do Vitta Beauty para alterar. Entre
        usando o Google ou use “Esqueci minha senha” caso tenha adicionado
        acesso por e-mail.
      </p>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <FormFeedback state={updateState} />

      {!verified ? (
        <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={verifyCurrentPassword}>
          <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
            Confirme sua senha atual para continuar. Depois da confirmação, você
            poderá definir uma nova senha sem sair do Vitta Beauty.
          </p>
          <PasswordInput
            autoComplete="current-password"
            id="current-password"
            label="Senha Atual"
            maxLength={72}
            name="currentPassword"
            onChange={setCurrentPassword}
            value={currentPassword}
          />
          <FormFeedback state={verifyState} />
          <button
            className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-white shadow-[0_8px_18px_rgba(121,80,165,0.2)] transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60 sm:w-auto sm:min-w-44"
            disabled={pending}
            type="submit"
          >
            {pending ? "Verificando..." : "Verificar"}
          </button>
        </form>
      ) : (
        <form
          className="space-y-5"
          onSubmit={updatePassword}
        >
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-800">
            Senha atual confirmada. Informe a nova senha para atualizar sua
            conta.
          </div>

          <PasswordInput
            autoComplete="new-password"
            id="new-password"
            label="Nova Senha"
            maxLength={72}
            name="newPassword"
            onChange={setNewPassword}
            value={newPassword}
          />
          <ul aria-live="polite" className="grid gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
            {requirements.map((requirement) => (
              <li
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 ${
                  requirement.met ? "text-emerald-700" : "text-vitta-muted"
                }`}
                key={requirement.label}
              >
                <span
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className={`flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
                    requirement.met
                      ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"
                      : "bg-[#f0edf2] text-vitta-muted"
                  }`}
                >
                  {requirement.met ? "✓" : "·"}
                </span>
                {requirement.label}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>

          <div>
            <PasswordInput
              autoComplete="new-password"
              id="confirm-new-password"
              label="Confirmar Nova Senha"
              maxLength={72}
              name="confirmPassword"
              onChange={setConfirmPassword}
              value={confirmPassword}
            />
            {confirmPassword && (
              <p
                className={`mt-2 text-xs ${
                  passwordsMatch ? "text-emerald-700" : "text-rose-700"
                }`}
                role="status"
              >
                {passwordsMatch
                  ? "As senhas coincidem."
                  : "As senhas ainda não coincidem."}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
            <button
              className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
              onClick={() => {
                setUnlocked(false);
                setVerifyState(initialState);
                setCurrentPassword("");
                setNewPassword("");
                setConfirmPassword("");
              }}
              type="button"
            >
              Recomeçar verificação
            </button>
            <button
              className="rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-white shadow-[0_8px_18px_rgba(121,80,165,0.2)] transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              disabled={
                pending ||
                requirements.some((requirement) => !requirement.met) ||
                !passwordsMatch
              }
              type="submit"
            >
              {pending ? "Atualizando..." : "Atualizar Senha"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\auth\auth-form.tsx ---
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, useTransition, type FormEvent } from "react";
import {
  createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
  sendEmailVerification,
  signInWithEmailAndPassword,
  signInWithPopup,
  type User,
} from "firebase/auth";
import {
  linkLegacyFirebaseAccountAction,
  loginWithFirebaseAction,
  verifyLegacyCredentialsAction,
} from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { PasswordInput } from "@/components/forms/password-input";
import { getFirebaseActionCodeSettings } from "@/lib/firebase-action-settings";
import { auth, googleProvider } from "@/lib/firebase";

type AuthFormProps = {
  accountDeletionPending?: boolean;
  registered?: boolean;
  initialEmail?: string;
};

function firebaseErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  const code =
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "code" in error &&
    typeof error.code === "string"
      ? error.code
      : "";

  if (
    code === "auth/invalid-credential" ||
    code === "auth/user-not-found" ||
    code === "auth/wrong-password"
  ) {
    return "E-mail ou senha inválidos.";
  }
  if (code === "auth/too-many-requests") {
    return "Muitas tentativas. Aguarde um pouco e tente novamente.";
  }
  if (code === "auth/popup-closed-by-user") {
    return "A janela de autenticação foi fechada antes de concluir.";
  }
  if (code === "auth/account-exists-with-different-credential") {
    return "Já existe uma conta com este e-mail. Entre usando o método cadastrado.";
  }
  if (code === "auth/network-request-failed") {
    return "Falha de conexão. Verifique sua internet e tente novamente.";
  }
  return "Não foi possível entrar. Verifique seus dados e tente novamente.";
}

export function AuthForm({
  accountDeletionPending = false,
  registered = false,
  initialEmail = "",
}: AuthFormProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(initialEmail);
  const [loginPassword, setLoginPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [verificationUser, setVerificationUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  async function completeSignIn(user: User) {
    const usesGoogleProvider = user.providerData.some(
      (provider) => provider.providerId === "google.com",
    );
    if (!user.emailVerified && !usesGoogleProvider) {
      setVerificationUser(user);
      setError(
        "Seu e-mail ainda precisa ser confirmado. Use o botão abaixo para reenviar a confirmação.",
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      const result = await loginWithFirebaseAction(await user.getIdToken(true));
      if (result.registrationRequired && result.email) {
        const emailQuery = encodeURIComponent(result.email);
        router.push(
          result.googleRegistration
            ? `/cadastro/complemento?email=${emailQuery}`
            : `/cadastro?email=${emailQuery}`,
        );
        return;
      }
      if (result.error) {
        setError(result.error);
        return;
      }
      if (result.success) {
        router.replace("/dashboard");
        router.refresh();
        return;
      }
      setError("Não foi possível iniciar a sessão. Tente novamente.");
    } catch (signInError) {
      setError("Não foi possível validar sua conta. Tente novamente.");
      console.error("Firebase login client flow failed.", signInError);
    }
  }

  function submitEmailPassword(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const submittedEmail = String(formData.get("email") ?? "")
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
    const password = formData.get("password");
    if (typeof password !== "string") {
      setError("Informe seu e-mail e sua senha.");
      return;
    }

    setEmail(submittedEmail);
    setError("");
    setStatus("");
    setVerificationUser(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      let user: User;
      try {
        const credential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(
          auth,
          submittedEmail,
          password,
        );
        user = credential.user;
      } catch (authError) {
        const code =
          typeof authError === "object" &&
          authError !== null &&
          "code" in authError &&
          typeof authError.code === "string"
            ? authError.code
            : "";
        if (
          code === "auth/user-not-found" ||
          code === "auth/invalid-credential"
        ) {
          try {
            const isLegacyAccount = await verifyLegacyCredentialsAction(
              submittedEmail,
              password,
            );
            if (isLegacyAccount) {
              const credential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
                auth,
                submittedEmail,
                password,
              );
              await sendEmailVerification(
                credential.user,
                getFirebaseActionCodeSettings(),
              );
              const result = await linkLegacyFirebaseAccountAction(
                await credential.user.getIdToken(true),
              );
              if (result.error) {
                setError(result.error);
                return;
              }
              setVerificationUser(credential.user);
              setError(
                "Sua conta foi migrada. Confirme o e-mail enviado para liberar o acesso.",
              );
              return;
            }
          } catch (migrationError) {
            setError(firebaseErrorMessage(migrationError));
            return;
          }
        }
        setError(firebaseErrorMessage(authError));
        return;
      }
      await completeSignIn(user);
    });
  }

  function submitGoogle() {
    setError("");
    setStatus("");
    startTransition(async () => {
      let user: User;
      try {
        const credential = await signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
        user = credential.user;
      } catch (authError) {
        setError(firebaseErrorMessage(authError));
        console.error("Firebase Google popup failed.", authError);
        return;
      }
      await completeSignIn(user);
    });
  }

  function resendVerification() {
    if (!verificationUser) return;
    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        await sendEmailVerification(
          verificationUser,
          getFirebaseActionCodeSettings(),
        );
        setStatus(
          `Enviamos novamente o e-mail de confirmação para ${verificationUser.email}.`,
        );
      } catch (authError) {
        setError(firebaseErrorMessage(authError));
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <section className="w-full max-w-md rounded-[28px] border border-white/80 bg-white/90 p-7 shadow-[0_24px_70px_rgba(66,47,78,0.12)] backdrop-blur-xl sm:p-9">
      <header className="mb-8">
        <div className="relative mb-3 h-[160px] w-[300px] max-w-full overflow-hidden">
          <Image
            alt="Vitta Beauty"
            className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-[267px] w-[400px] max-w-none -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-[40%]"
            height={408}
            priority
            src="/brand/logo_vitta.png"
            width={612}
          />
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-[28px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.04em] text-vitta-text">
          Bem-vinda de volta
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
          Entre para acessar seu espaço de gestão.
        </p>
      </header>

      {registered && (
        <p
          className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-6 text-emerald-800"
          role="status"
        >
          Cadastro realizado! O suporte do Vitta Beauty enviou um e-mail de
          confirmação para você. Por favor, confirme seu e-mail antes de
          realizar o login.
        </p>
      )}
      {accountDeletionPending && (
        <p
          className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-900"
          role="alert"
        >
          A autenticação foi removida, mas a exclusão dos dados não foi
          concluída. Entre em contato com o suporte para finalizar a solicitação.
        </p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p
          className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-rose-800"
          role="alert"
        >
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      {status && (
        <p
          className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-800"
          role="status"
        >
          {status}
        </p>
      )}

      {verificationUser && (
        <button
          className="mb-5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta transition hover:bg-vitta/5 disabled:opacity-60"
          disabled={pending}
          onClick={resendVerification}
          type="button"
        >
          Reenviar e-mail de confirmação
        </button>
      )}

      <button
        className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-white px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background disabled:opacity-60"
        disabled={pending}
        onClick={submitGoogle}
        type="button"
      >
        <svg aria-hidden="true" className="h-5 w-5" viewBox="0 0 48 48">
          <path fill="#EA4335" d="M24 9.5c3.54 0 6.71 1.22 9.21 3.6l6.85-6.85C35.9 2.38 30.47 0 24 0 14.62 0 6.51 5.38 2.56 13.22l7.98 6.19C12.43 13.72 17.74 9.5 24 9.5Z" transform="translate(0 4)" />
          <path fill="#4285F4" d="M46.98 24.55c0-1.57-.15-3.09-.38-4.55H24v9.02h12.94c-.58 2.96-2.26 5.48-4.76 7.18l7.73 6C44.42 38.05 46.98 31.9 46.98 24.55Z" />
          <path fill="#FBBC05" d="M10.53 28.59a14.4 14.4 0 0 1 0-9.18l-7.98-6.19a23.9 23.9 0 0 0 0 21.56l7.98-6.19Z" transform="translate(0 4)" />
          <path fill="#34A853" d="M24 48c6.48 0 11.93-2.13 15.91-5.8l-7.73-6c-2.14 1.45-4.89 2.3-8.18 2.3-6.26 0-11.57-4.22-13.47-9.91l-7.98 6.19C6.51 42.62 14.62 48 24 48Z" transform="translate(0 -4)" />
        </svg>
        Entrar com Google
      </button>

      <div className="my-5 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-vitta-muted">
        <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-vitta-border" />
        ou entre com e-mail
        <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-vitta-border" />
      </div>

      <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submitEmailPassword}>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          E-mail
          <input
            autoComplete="email"
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#fcfbfd] px-4 py-3.5 text-vitta-text outline-none transition"
            maxLength={254}
            name="email"
            onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.currentTarget.value)}
            required
            type="email"
            value={email}
          />
        </label>

        <div>
          <PasswordInput
            autoComplete="current-password"
            id="auth-password"
            label="Senha"
            name="password"
            onChange={setLoginPassword}
            value={loginPassword}
          />
          <Link
            className="mt-2 inline-block text-xs font-semibold text-vitta hover:underline"
            href="/esqueci-senha"
          >
            Esqueci minha senha
          </Link>
        </div>

        <button
          className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-white shadow-[0_8px_18px_rgba(121,80,165,0.2)] transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
          disabled={pending}
          type="submit"
        >
          {pending ? "Aguarde..." : "Entrar"}
        </button>
      </form>

      <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-vitta-muted">
        Ainda não tem uma conta?{" "}
        <Link
          className="font-semibold text-vitta underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
          href="/cadastro"
        >
          Cadastre-se
        </Link>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\auth\logout-button.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, useTransition, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { logoutAction } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { auth } from "@/lib/firebase";
import { signOut } from "firebase/auth";

export function LogoutButton({
  children,
  className,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  className: string;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  function logout() {
    setError("");
    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        await logoutAction();
      } catch (logoutError) {
        console.error("Could not clear the Firebase session cookie.", logoutError);
        setError("Não foi possível encerrar a sessão. Tente novamente.");
        return;
      }

      try {
        await signOut(auth);
      } catch (logoutError) {
        console.error("Could not sign out of Firebase in the browser.", logoutError);
        setError(
          "A sessão do app foi encerrada, mas não foi possível limpar a sessão Firebase deste navegador.",
        );
      }
      router.replace("/login");
      router.refresh();
    });
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button
        className={className}
        disabled={pending}
        onClick={logout}
        type="button"
      >
        {pending ? "Saindo..." : children}
      </button>
      {error && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-rose-700" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\auth\password-reset-form.tsx ---
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { sendPasswordResetEmail } from "firebase/auth";
import { getFirebaseActionCodeSettings } from "@/lib/firebase-action-settings";
import { auth } from "@/lib/firebase";

export function PasswordResetForm() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

  async function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPending(true);
    setError("");
    setSent(false);
    try {
      await sendPasswordResetEmail(
        auth,
        email.trim().toLowerCase(),
        getFirebaseActionCodeSettings(),
      );
      setSent(true);
    } catch (resetError) {
      const code =
        typeof resetError === "object" &&
        resetError !== null &&
        "code" in resetError &&
        typeof resetError.code === "string"
          ? resetError.code
          : "";
      setError(
        code === "auth/invalid-email"
          ? "Informe um e-mail válido."
          : code === "auth/network-request-failed"
            ? "Falha de conexão. Verifique sua internet e tente novamente."
            : "Não foi possível enviar as instruções. Confira o e-mail e tente novamente.",
      );
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className="w-full max-w-md rounded-[28px] border border-white/80 bg-white/90 p-7 shadow-[0_24px_70px_rgba(66,47,78,0.12)] backdrop-blur-xl sm:p-9">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-vitta/75">
        Acesso à conta
      </p>
      <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-vitta-text">
        Esqueci minha senha
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
        Informe o e-mail cadastrado para receber as instruções de redefinição.
      </p>
      {sent && (
        <p
          className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-800"
          role="status"
        >
          Se houver uma conta com esse e-mail, as instruções para redefinir a
          senha foram enviadas.
        </p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p
          className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-rose-800"
          role="alert"
        >
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <form className="mt-6 space-y-4" onSubmit={submit}>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          E-mail
          <input
            autoComplete="email"
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#fcfbfd] px-4 py-3.5 text-vitta-text outline-none"
            onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.currentTarget.value)}
            required
            type="email"
            value={email}
          />
        </label>
        <button
          className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-60"
          disabled={pending}
          type="submit"
        >
          {pending ? "Enviando..." : "Enviar instruções"}
        </button>
      </form>
      <Link
        className="mt-6 block text-center text-sm font-semibold text-vitta hover:underline"
        href="/login"
      >
        Voltar ao login
      </Link>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\auth\registration-wizard.tsx ---
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useActionState, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import {
  createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
  sendEmailVerification,
  signOut,
} from "firebase/auth";
import {
  registerAction,
  type FormState,
} from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { PasswordInput } from "@/components/forms/password-input";
import {
  formatBrazilianPhone,
  formatCpf,
  getPasswordRequirements,
  hasValidProfessionalName,
  isValidCpf,
} from "@/lib/professional-validation";
import { getFirebaseActionCodeSettings } from "@/lib/firebase-action-settings";
import { auth } from "@/lib/firebase";

type RegistrationDetails = {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  cpf: string;
  phone: string;
  specialty: string;
};

const initialState: FormState = {};
const inputClass =
  "mt-2 w-full rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#fcfbfd] px-4 py-3.5 text-vitta-text outline-none transition";

function firebaseErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  const code =
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    "code" in error &&
    typeof error.code === "string"
      ? error.code
      : "";

  if (code === "auth/email-already-in-use") {
    return "Este e-mail já possui uma conta Firebase. Entre ou redefina sua senha.";
  }
  if (code === "auth/invalid-email") return "Informe um e-mail válido.";
  if (code === "auth/weak-password") {
    return "A senha não atende aos requisitos de segurança.";
  }
  if (code === "auth/network-request-failed") {
    return "Falha de conexão. Verifique sua internet e tente novamente.";
  }
  return "Não foi possível concluir o cadastro. Tente novamente.";
}

function TermsAcceptance({
  checked,
  onChange,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <label className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e3ed] bg-[#fcfbfd] p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-text">
      <input
        checked={checked}
        className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 accent-[#7950a5]"
        name="acceptedTerms"
        onChange={(event) => onChange(event.currentTarget.checked)}
        required
        type="checkbox"
        value="true"
      />
      <span>
        Li e concordo com os{" "}
        <a
          className="font-semibold text-vitta underline decoration-vitta/30 underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-vitta"
          href="/termos"
          rel="noreferrer"
          target="_blank"
        >
          Termos de Uso
        </a>{" "}
        e a{" "}
        <a
          className="font-semibold text-vitta underline decoration-vitta/30 underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-vitta"
          href="/termos"
          rel="noreferrer"
          target="_blank"
        >
          Política de Privacidade
        </a>
        .
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

export function RegistrationWizard({
  initialEmail = "",
  googleRegistration = false,
}: {
  initialEmail?: string;
  googleRegistration?: boolean;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<1 | 2>(1);
  const [details, setDetails] = useState<RegistrationDetails>({
    name: "",
    email: initialEmail,
    cpf: "",
    phone: "",
    specialty: "",
  });
  const [nameError, setNameError] = useState("");
  const [cpfError, setCpfError] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [termsAccepted, setTermsAccepted] = useState(false);
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(
    async (previousState: FormState, formData: FormData) => {
      const email = String(formData.get("email") ?? "")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase();
      const submittedPassword = formData.get("password");
      const submittedConfirmation = formData.get("confirmPassword");
      if (formData.get("acceptedTerms") !== "true") {
        return {
          error:
            "Leia e aceite os Termos de Uso e a Política de Privacidade.",
        };
      }
      if (
        !googleRegistration &&
        (typeof submittedPassword !== "string" ||
          submittedPassword !== submittedConfirmation)
      ) {
        return { error: "As senhas informadas não coincidem." };
      }
      let user = auth.currentUser;

      try {
        if (googleRegistration) {
          if (!user || user.email?.toLowerCase() !== email) {
            return {
              error:
                "Sua sessão Google expirou. Volte ao login e entre com Google novamente.",
            };
          }
        } else {
          if (!user || user.email?.toLowerCase() !== email) {
            if (typeof submittedPassword !== "string") {
              return { error: "Informe uma senha válida." };
            }
            const credential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
              auth,
              email,
              submittedPassword,
            );
            user = credential.user;
          }

          await sendEmailVerification(user, getFirebaseActionCodeSettings());
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Firebase registration client flow failed.", error);
        return { error: firebaseErrorMessage(error) };
      }

      if (!user) {
        return {
          error:
            "Sua sessão Firebase expirou. Volte ao login e tente novamente.",
        };
      }

      let result: FormState;
      try {
        const idToken = await user.getIdToken(true);
        formData.set("authMethod", googleRegistration ? "google" : "password");
        formData.set("firebaseIdToken", idToken);
        result = await registerAction(previousState, formData);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Registration server action failed.", error);
        return {
          error: "Não foi possível concluir o cadastro. Tente novamente.",
        };
      }
      if (result.error) return result;
      if (googleRegistration) {
        router.replace("/dashboard");
        router.refresh();
        return result;
      }

      try {
        await signOut(auth);
      } catch {
        console.error("Firebase sign-out after registration failed.");
        return {
          error:
            "Cadastro concluído, mas não foi possível encerrar a sessão. Saia da conta e entre após confirmar o e-mail.",
        };
      }
      router.replace(
        `/login?registered=1&email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}`,
      );
      return result;
    },
    initialState,
  );
  const passwordRequirements = getPasswordRequirements(password);
  const passwordsMatch = password === confirmPassword;
  const passwordMismatch = confirmPassword.length > 0 && !passwordsMatch;

  function updateDetail<K extends keyof RegistrationDetails>(
    key: K,
    value: RegistrationDetails[K],
  ) {
    setDetails((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }));
  }

  function validateProfile(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    if (step === 2 && !googleRegistration) {
      if (
        passwordRequirements.some((requirement) => !requirement.met) ||
        !passwordsMatch ||
        !termsAccepted
      ) {
        event.preventDefault();
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!hasValidProfessionalName(details.name)) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setNameError("Informe seu nome e sobrenome ou o nome do negócio.");
      return;
    }
    setNameError("");
    if (!isValidCpf(details.cpf)) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setCpfError("Confira os números: este CPF não parece válido.");
      return;
    }
    setCpfError("");
    if (googleRegistration && !termsAccepted) {
      event.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
    if (!googleRegistration) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setStep(2);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className="w-full max-w-xl rounded-[28px] border border-white/80 bg-white/90 p-6 shadow-[0_24px_70px_rgba(66,47,78,0.12)] backdrop-blur-xl sm:p-9">
      <header className="mb-8 text-center">
        <div className="relative mx-auto mb-2 h-[150px] w-[280px] max-w-full overflow-hidden">
          <Image
            alt="Vitta Beauty"
            className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-[250px] w-[375px] max-w-none -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-[40%]"
            height={408}
            priority
            src="/brand/logo_vitta.png"
            width={612}
          />
        </div>
        <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-vitta/75">
          Seu espaço profissional
        </p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 text-[27px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.04em] text-vitta-text">
          {step === 1 ? "Vamos conhecer seu negócio" : "Proteja sua conta"}
        </h1>
        <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-vitta-muted">
          {googleRegistration
            ? "Complete seu perfil para acessar seu espaço profissional."
            : step === 1
              ? "Conte um pouco sobre você e o seu espaço de beleza."
              : "Crie uma senha forte. Enviaremos uma confirmação para seu e-mail."}
        </p>
      </header>

      <form
        action={formAction}
        className="space-y-5 text-left"
        onSubmit={validateProfile}
      >
        {step === 2 && !googleRegistration ? (
          <>
            <input name="name" type="hidden" value={details.name} />
            <input name="cpf" type="hidden" value={details.cpf} />
            <input name="phone" type="hidden" value={details.phone} />
            <input name="specialty" type="hidden" value={details.specialty} />
            <input name="email" type="hidden" value={details.email} />
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background/70 px-4 py-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-medium text-vitta-muted">
                Cadastro para
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                {details.name}
              </p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 break-words text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                {details.email}
              </p>
            </div>
            <PasswordInput
              autoComplete="new-password"
              id="registration-password"
              label="Crie sua senha"
              maxLength={72}
              minLength={8}
              name="password"
              onChange={setPassword}
              value={password}
            />
            <div>
              <PasswordInput
                autoComplete="new-password"
                id="registration-confirm-password"
                label="Confirmar Senha"
                maxLength={72}
                minLength={8}
                name="confirmPassword"
                onChange={setConfirmPassword}
                value={confirmPassword}
              />
              {passwordMismatch && (
                <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-rose-700" role="alert">
                  As senhas não coincidem. Confira os dois campos.
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
            <ul aria-live="polite" className="grid gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
              {passwordRequirements.map((requirement) => (
                <li
                  className={
                    requirement.met ? "text-emerald-700" : "text-vitta-muted"
                  }
                  key={requirement.label}
                >
                  {requirement.met ? "✓ " : "· "}
                  {requirement.label}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <TermsAcceptance
              checked={termsAccepted}
              onChange={setTermsAccepted}
            />
            {state.error && (
              <p className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-rose-800" role="alert">
                {state.error}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <button
                className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text"
                onClick={() => setStep(1)}
                type="button"
              >
                Voltar aos dados
              </button>
              <button
                className="rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-60"
                disabled={
                  pending ||
                  passwordRequirements.some((requirement) => !requirement.met) ||
                  !passwordsMatch ||
                  !termsAccepted
                }
                formNoValidate
                onClick={(event) => {
                  if (
                    passwordRequirements.some(
                      (requirement) => !requirement.met,
                    ) ||
                    !passwordsMatch ||
                    !termsAccepted
                  ) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                  }
                }}
                type="submit"
              >
                {pending ? "Criando conta..." : "Criar minha conta"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              Nome profissional ou do negócio
              <input
                autoComplete="organization"
                className={inputClass}
                maxLength={80}
                name="name"
                onChange={(event) => {
                  updateDetail("name", event.currentTarget.value);
                  setNameError("");
                }}
                placeholder="Ex.: Ana HairStudio ou Ana Santos"
                required
                value={details.name}
              />
              {nameError && (
                <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-rose-700">
                  {nameError}
                </span>
              )}
            </label>
            <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
                CPF
                <input
                  autoComplete="off"
                  className={inputClass}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  maxLength={14}
                  name="cpf"
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    updateDetail("cpf", formatCpf(event.currentTarget.value));
                    setCpfError("");
                  }}
                  placeholder="000.000.000-00"
                  required
                  value={details.cpf}
                />
                {cpfError && (
                  <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-rose-700">
                    {cpfError}
                  </span>
                )}
              </label>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
                Telefone / WhatsApp
                <input
                  autoComplete="tel"
                  className={inputClass}
                  inputMode="tel"
                  maxLength={16}
                  name="phone"
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateDetail(
                      "phone",
                      formatBrazilianPhone(event.currentTarget.value),
                    )
                  }
                  placeholder="(11) 99999-9999"
                  required
                  type="tel"
                  value={details.phone}
                />
              </label>
            </div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              Especialidade
              <input
                autoComplete="organization-title"
                className={inputClass}
                maxLength={80}
                name="specialty"
                onChange={(event) =>
                  updateDetail("specialty", event.currentTarget.value)
                }
                placeholder="Ex.: Manicure, Esteticista, Cabeleireira"
                required
                value={details.specialty}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              E-mail de acesso
              <input
                autoComplete="email"
                className={`${inputClass} ${googleRegistration ? "cursor-not-allowed bg-[#f3f0f5] text-vitta-muted" : ""}`}
                maxLength={254}
                name="email"
                onChange={(event) => updateDetail("email", event.target.value)}
                readOnly={googleRegistration}
                required
                type="email"
                value={details.email}
              />
            </label>
            {googleRegistration && (
              <TermsAcceptance
                checked={termsAccepted}
                onChange={setTermsAccepted}
              />
            )}
            {state.error && (
              <p className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-rose-800" role="alert">
                {state.error}
              </p>
            )}
            <button
              className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#7950a5] to-[#9368b3] px-5 py-3.5 font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-60"
              disabled={pending || (googleRegistration && !termsAccepted)}
              type="submit"
            >
              {pending
                ? "Criando conta..."
                : googleRegistration
                  ? "Concluir cadastro"
                  : "Continuar para criar senha"}
            </button>
          </>
        )}
      </form>

      <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-vitta-muted">
        Já tem uma conta?{" "}
        <Link
          className="font-semibold text-vitta underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
          href="/login"
        >
          Entrar
        </Link>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\booking\copy-booking-link.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

export function CopyBookingLink({ url }: { url: string }) {
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function copyLink() {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      setMessage("Link copiado.");
    } catch {
      setMessage("Não foi possível copiar automaticamente. Selecione e copie o link.");
    }
  }

  const shareUrl = `https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(
    `Olá! Agende seu horário comigo: ${url}`,
  )}`;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text" htmlFor="booking-link">
        Seu link exclusivo
      </label>
      <input
        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-vitta-background px-4 py-3 text-sm text-vitta-text outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
        id="booking-link"
        readOnly
        value={url}
        onFocus={(event) => event.currentTarget.select()}
      />
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <button
          className="rounded-xl bg-vitta px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-vitta/90"
          onClick={copyLink}
          type="button"
        >
          Copiar link
        </button>
        <a
          className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
          href={shareUrl}
          rel="noreferrer"
          target="_blank"
        >
          Compartilhar pelo WhatsApp
        </a>
        <a
          className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
          href={url}
          rel="noreferrer"
          target="_blank"
        >
          Abrir página pública
        </a>
      </div>
      <p aria-live="polite" className="min-h-5 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
        {message}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { createPublicBookingAction } from "@/app/actions/public-booking";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { FormFeedback } from "@/components/forms/form-feedback";
import { formatTime } from "@/lib/schedule";
import type { AvailableSlot } from "@/lib/public-booking";

type PublicBookingFormProps = {
  slug: string;
  serviceId: string;
  date: string;
  timeZone: string;
  slots: AvailableSlot[];
};

const initialState: FormState = {};

export function PublicBookingForm({
  slug,
  serviceId,
  date,
  timeZone,
  slots,
}: PublicBookingFormProps) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(
    createPublicBookingAction,
    initialState,
  );

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-5">
      <input name="slug" type="hidden" value={slug} />
      <input name="serviceId" type="hidden" value={serviceId} />
      <input name="date" type="hidden" value={date} />

      <fieldset>
        <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text">
          Escolha um horário
        </legend>
        {slots.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-vitta-background p-4 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
            Não há horários disponíveis para este serviço nesta data. Escolha
            outro dia para consultar a agenda.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
            {slots.map((slot) => (
              <label className="cursor-pointer" key={slot.value}>
                <input
                  className="peer sr-only"
                  name="startsAt"
                  required
                  type="radio"
                  value={slot.value}
                />
                <span className="block rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text transition hover:border-vitta peer-checked:border-vitta peer-checked:bg-vitta peer-checked:text-white peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-vitta peer-focus-visible:ring-offset-2">
                  {formatTime(slot.startsAt, timeZone)}
                </span>
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </fieldset>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          Seu nome
          <input
            autoComplete="name"
            className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-3 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
            maxLength={100}
            name="name"
            required
          />
        </label>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
          WhatsApp com DDD
          <input
            autoComplete="tel"
            className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-3 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
            inputMode="tel"
            maxLength={24}
            name="whatsapp"
            placeholder="(11) 99999-9999"
            required
            type="tel"
          />
        </label>
      </div>

      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
        Observações (opcional)
        <textarea
          className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-3 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
          maxLength={500}
          name="notes"
          rows={3}
        />
      </label>

      <FormFeedback state={state} />
      <button
        className="w-full rounded-xl bg-vitta px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-vitta/90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
        disabled={pending || slots.length === 0}
        type="submit"
      >
        {pending ? "Enviando solicitação..." : "Solicitar agendamento"}
      </button>
      <p className="text-center text-xs leading-5 text-vitta-muted">
        O horário será solicitado à profissional e precisa ser confirmado.
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { updateAppointmentStatusAction } from "@/app/actions/calendar";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { FormFeedback } from "@/components/forms/form-feedback";

const initialState: FormState = {};

export function AppointmentControls({
  appointmentId,
  startsAt,
  status,
}: {
  appointmentId: string;
  startsAt: Date;
  status: "PENDING" | "CONFIRMED";
}) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(
    updateAppointmentStatusAction,
    initialState,
  );

  return (
    <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
      <FormFeedback state={state} />
      <form action={formAction} className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <input name="appointmentId" type="hidden" value={appointmentId} />
        {status === "PENDING" && (
          <button
            className="rounded-lg bg-vitta px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-vitta/90 disabled:opacity-60"
            disabled={pending}
            name="intent"
            type="submit"
            value="confirm"
          >
            Confirmar horário
          </button>
        )}
        {status === "CONFIRMED" && startsAt <= new Date() && (
          <button
            className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background disabled:opacity-60"
            disabled={pending}
            name="intent"
            type="submit"
            value="complete"
          >
            Concluir
          </button>
        )}
        <button
          className="rounded-lg border border-rose-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-rose-700 transition hover:bg-rose-50 disabled:opacity-60"
          disabled={pending}
          name="intent"
          type="submit"
          value="cancel"
        >
          Cancelar
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\calendar\delete-calendar-block-form.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { deleteCalendarBlockAction } from "@/app/actions/calendar";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { FormFeedback } from "@/components/forms/form-feedback";

const initialState: FormState = {};

export function DeleteCalendarBlockForm({ blockId }: { blockId: string }) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(
    deleteCalendarBlockAction,
    initialState,
  );

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="mt-3 space-y-2">
      <input name="blockId" type="hidden" value={blockId} />
      <FormFeedback state={state} />
      <button
        className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-white disabled:opacity-60"
        disabled={pending}
        type="submit"
      >
        {pending ? "Removendo..." : "Remover bloqueio"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\calendar\schedule-forms.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { createAppointmentAction } from "@/app/actions/calendar";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { FormFeedback } from "@/components/forms/form-feedback";

const initialState: FormState = {};

type ScheduleFormsProps = {
  clients: Array<{ id: string; name: string; whatsapp: string }>;
  services: Array<{ id: string; name: string; durationMinutes: number }>;
};

export function ScheduleForms({ clients, services }: ScheduleFormsProps) {
  const [appointmentState, appointmentAction, appointmentPending] =
    useActionState(createAppointmentAction, initialState);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <details className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white shadow-card">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer list-none p-5 sm:p-6">
          <span className="block font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Novo atendimento
          </span>
          <span className="mt-1 block text-sm text-vitta-muted">
            Agende um horário para um cliente cadastrado.
          </span>
        </summary>
        <form action={appointmentAction} className="space-y-4 border-t border-vitta-border p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              Cliente
              <select
                className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-2.5 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
                name="clientId"
                required
              >
                <option value="">Selecione um cliente</option>
                {clients.map((client) => (
                  <option key={client.id} value={client.id}>
                    {client.name} · {client.whatsapp}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              Serviço
              <select
                className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-2.5 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
                name="serviceId"
                required
              >
                <option value="">Selecione um serviço</option>
                {services.map((service) => (
                  <option key={service.id} value={service.id}>
                    {service.name} · {service.durationMinutes} min
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
            Início
            <input
              className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
              name="startsAt"
              required
              type="datetime-local"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
            Observações
            <textarea
              className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5 outline-none focus:border-vitta focus:ring-2 focus:ring-vitta/15"
              maxLength={1000}
              name="notes"
              rows={2}
            />
          </label>
          {clients.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-sm text-amber-800">
              Cadastre um cliente antes de criar um atendimento.
            </p>
          )}
          {services.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-sm text-amber-800">
              Cadastre um serviço ativo antes de criar um atendimento.
            </p>
          )}
          <FormFeedback state={appointmentState} />
          <button
            className="rounded-xl bg-vitta px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-vitta/90 disabled:opacity-60"
            disabled={
              appointmentPending || clients.length === 0 || services.length === 0
            }
            type="submit"
          >
            {appointmentPending ? "Agendando..." : "Agendar atendimento"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </details>

    </div>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\calendar\whatsapp-shortcuts.tsx ---
import { formatTime } from "@/lib/schedule";
import type { AppointmentStatus } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { getWhatsAppNumber } from "@/lib/whatsapp";

type WhatsAppShortcutsProps = {
  whatsapp: string;
  clientName: string;
  serviceName: string;
  startsAt: Date;
  endsAt: Date;
  timeZone: string;
  professionalName: string;
  status: AppointmentStatus;
};

export function WhatsAppShortcuts({
  whatsapp,
  clientName,
  serviceName,
  startsAt,
  endsAt,
  timeZone,
  professionalName,
  status,
}: WhatsAppShortcutsProps) {
  const date = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    timeZone,
    dateStyle: "long",
  }).format(startsAt);
  const time = `${formatTime(startsAt, timeZone)} às ${formatTime(endsAt, timeZone)}`;
  const messages =
    status === "PENDING"
      ? [
          {
            label: "Solicitação recebida",
            message: `Olá, ${clientName}! Recebi sua solicitação para ${serviceName}, em ${date}, das ${time}. Vou confirmar seu horário em breve.`,
          },
        ]
      : status === "CONFIRMED"
        ? [
            {
              label: "Confirmação",
              message: `Olá, ${clientName}! Seu horário para ${serviceName}, em ${date}, das ${time}, está confirmado. Até lá!`,
            },
            {
              label: "Lembrete",
              message: `Oi, ${clientName}! Passando para lembrar do seu horário de ${serviceName}, em ${date}, das ${time}. Até breve!`,
            },
          ]
        : status === "COMPLETED"
          ? [
              {
                label: "Agradecimento",
                message: `Oi, ${clientName}! Obrigada pela preferência e por escolher ${professionalName}. Foi um prazer te atender!`,
              },
            ]
          : [];
  const phone = getWhatsAppNumber(whatsapp);

  return (
    <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
      {messages.map((item) => (
        <a
          className="rounded-lg border border-vitta-border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-vitta-text transition hover:bg-vitta-background"
          href={`https://wa.me/${phone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(item.message)}`}
          key={item.label}
          rel="noreferrer"
          target="_blank"
        >
          WhatsApp · {item.label}
        </a>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

--- ARQUIVO: src\components\calendar\working-hours-settings.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useActionState, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { saveWorkingHoursRulesAction } from "@/app/actions/calendar";
import type { FormState } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { FormFeedback } from "@/components/forms/form-feedback";

const initialState: FormState = {};
const weekdays = [
  "Domingo",
  "Segunda-feira",
  "Terça-feira",
  "Quarta-feira",
  "Quinta-feira",
  "Sexta-feira",
  "Sábado",
];
const intervals = [15, 30, 45, 60] as const;

type WorkingHour = {
  weekday: number;
  startMinute: number;
  endMinute: number;
  interval: number;
  blockedSlots: string[];
  isActive: boolean;
};

function minuteToTime(value: number): string {
  const hour = Math.floor(value / 60).toString().padStart(2, "0");
  const minute = (value % 60).toString().padStart(2, "0");
  return `${hour}:${minute}`;
}

function timeToMinute(value: string): number | null {
  const match = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)$/.exec(value);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}

export function WorkingHoursSettings({
  workingHours,
}: {
  workingHours: WorkingHour[];
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const initialSource =
    workingHours.find((hours) => hours.isActive) ?? workingHours[1];
  const [sourceWeekday, setSourceWeekday] = useState(initialSource.weekday);
  const [startTime, setStartTime] = useState(
    minuteToTime(initialSource.startMinute),
  );
  const [endTime, setEndTime] = useState(minuteToTime(initialSource.endMinute));
  const [interval, setInterval] = useState(initialSource.interval);
  const [blockedSlots, setBlockedSlots] = useState(
    initialSource.blockedSlots,
  );
  const [activeWeekdays, setActiveWeekdays] = useState(
    () =>
      new Set(
        workingHours
          .filter((hours) => hours.isActive)
          .map((hours) => hours.weekday),
      ),
  );
  const [applyTo, setApplyTo] = useState<"all" | "day">("all");
  const [targetWeekday, setTargetWeekday] = useState(
    initialSource.weekday,
  );
  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [state, saveAction, pending] = useActionState(
    async (previousState: FormState, formData: FormData) => {
      const result = await saveWorkingHoursRulesAction(previousState, formData);
      if (result.success) {
        setDialogOpen(false);
        if (applyTo === "day") {
          setActiveWeekdays((current) => new Set(current).add(targetWeekday));
          setSourceWeekday(targetWeekday);
        } else {
          const firstActiveWeekday = Array.from(activeWeekdays)[0];
          if (firstActiveWeekday !== undefined) {
            setSourceWeekday(firstActiveWeekday);
          }
        }
        router.refresh();
      }
      return result;
    },
    initialState,
  );

  const previewSlots = useMemo(() => {
    const startMinute = timeToMinute(startTime);
    const endMinute = timeToMinute(endTime);
    if (
      startMinute === null ||
      endMinute === null ||
      endMinute <= startMinute ||
      !intervals.includes(interval as (typeof intervals)[number])
    ) {
      return [];
    }

    const slots: string[] = [];
    for (
      let minute = startMinute;
      minute + interval <= endMinute;
      minute += interval
    ) {
      slots.push(minuteToTime(minute));
    }
    return slots;
  }, [startTime, endTime, interval]);

  const visibleBlockedSlots = blockedSlots.filter((slot) =>
    previewSlots.includes(slot),
  );

  function loadDay(weekday: number) {
    const hours = workingHours.find((item) => item.weekday === weekday);
    setSourceWeekday(weekday);
    setTargetWeekday(weekday);
    setStartTime(minuteToTime(hours?.startMinute ?? 9 * 60));
    setEndTime(minuteToTime(hours?.endMinute ?? 17 * 60));
    setInterval(hours?.interval ?? 15);
    setBlockedSlots(hours?.blockedSlots ?? []);
  }

  function toggleWeekday(weekday: number) {
    setActiveWeekdays((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (next.has(weekday)) next.delete(weekday);
      else next.add(weekday);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function toggleBlockedSlot(slot: string) {
    setBlockedSlots((current) =>
      current.includes(slot)
        ? current.filter((value) => value !== slot)
        : [...current, slot].sort(),
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <form
        action={saveAction}
        className="space-y-6"
        id="working-hours-form"
      >
        <input name="applyTo" type="hidden" value={applyTo} />
        <input name="weekday" type="hidden" value={targetWeekday} />
        <input
          name="blockedSlots"
          type="hidden"
          value={JSON.stringify(visibleBlockedSlots)}
        />
        {Array.from(activeWeekdays).map((weekday) => (
          <input
            key={weekday}
            name="activeWeekday"
            type="hidden"
            value={weekday}
          />
        ))}
        <input name="interval" type="hidden" value={interval} />
        <input name="startTime" type="hidden" value={startTime} />
        <input name="endTime" type="hidden" value={endTime} />

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Expediente e intervalo
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
            Defina a grade que será usada para oferecer horários de atendimento.
          </p>
          <label className="mt-4 block max-w-sm text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
            Carregar regras de um dia
            <select
              className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-2.5"
              onChange={(event) => loadDay(Number(event.target.value))}
              value={sourceWeekday}
            >
              {weekdays.map((name, weekday) => (
                <option key={weekday} value={weekday}>
                  {name}
                  {activeWeekdays.has(weekday) ? "" : " · fechado"}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              Início
              <input
                className="mt-1.5 block w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5"
                onChange={(event) => setStartTime(event.target.value)}
                required
                type="time"
                value={startTime}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              Fim
              <input
                className="mt-1.5 block w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5"
                onChange={(event) => setEndTime(event.target.value)}
                required
                type="time"
                value={endTime}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-vitta-text">
              Intervalo
              <select
                className="mt-1.5 block w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-2.5"
                onChange={(event) => setInterval(Number(event.target.value))}
                value={interval}
              >
                {intervals.map((minutes) => (
                  <option key={minutes} value={minutes}>
                    {minutes} minutos
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                Pausas recorrentes
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
                Selecione os horários que devem ficar indisponíveis.
              </p>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-vitta-muted">
              {visibleBlockedSlots.length} pausa(s) selecionada(s)
            </p>
          </div>
          {previewSlots.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-5 md:grid-cols-7">
              {previewSlots.map((slot) => {
                const blocked = visibleBlockedSlots.includes(slot);
                return (
                  <button
                    aria-pressed={blocked}
                    aria-label={`${slot}: ${
                      blocked ? "pausa recorrente" : "disponível"
                    }`}
                    className={`rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition ${
                      blocked
                        ? "border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 opacity-60"
                        : "border-vitta-border bg-white text-vitta-text hover:border-vitta hover:text-vitta"
                    }`}
                    key={slot}
                    onClick={() => toggleBlockedSlot(slot)}
                    type="button"
                  >
                    {slot}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900">
              Informe um horário final posterior ao início para visualizar a
              grade.
            </p>
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-vitta-border bg-white p-5 shadow-card sm:p-6">
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-vitta-text">
            Dias de funcionamento
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-vitta-muted">
            Marque os dias que devem permanecer abertos. Ao aplicar regras a
            todos, elas serão atualizadas somente nos dias marcados.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4">
            {weekdays.map((name, weekday) => (
              <label
                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-vitta-text"
                key={weekday}
              >
                <input
                  checked={activeWeekdays.has(weekday)}
                  className="h-4 w-4 accent-vitta"
                  onChange={() => toggleWeekday(weekday)}
                  type="checkbox"
                />
                {name}
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        <FormFeedback state={state} />
        <button
          className="rounded-xl bg-vitta px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-vitta/90"
          onClick={() => setDialogOpen(true)}
          type="button"
        >
          Salvar regras
        </button>
      </form>

      {dialogOpen && (
        <div
          aria-labelledby="apply-working-hours-title"
          aria-modal="true"
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4"
          role="dialog"
        >
          <section className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl">
            <h2
              className="text-lg font-semibold text-vitta-text"
              id="apply-working-hours-title"
            >
              Como deseja aplicar essas regras?
            </h2>
            <fieldset className="mt-5 space-y-3">
              <legend className="sr-only">Aplicação das regras</legend>
              <label className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-vitta-border p-3">
                <input
                  checked={applyTo === "all"}
                  className="mt-1 accent-vitta"
                  name="applyMode"
                  onChange={() => setApplyTo("all")}
                  type="radio"
                />
                <span>
                  <span className="block text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                    Todos os dias de funcionamento
                  </span>
                  <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                    Aplica o mesmo expediente, intervalo e pausas aos dias
                    marcados.
                  </span>
                </span>
              </label>
              <label className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-vitta-border p-3">
                <input
                  checked={applyTo === "day"}
                  className="mt-1 accent-vitta"
                  name="applyMode"
                  onChange={() => setApplyTo("day")}
                  type="radio"
                />
                <span className="block flex-1">
                  <span className="block text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text">
                    Dia específico
                  </span>
                  <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-vitta-muted">
                    Atualiza somente o dia selecionado.
                  </span>
                  {applyTo === "day" && (
                    <select
                      className="mt-3 w-full rounded-xl border border-vitta-border bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm"
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setTargetWeekday(Number(event.target.value))
                      }
                      value={targetWeekday}
                    >
                      {weekdays.map((name, weekday) => (
                        <option key={weekday} value={weekday}>
                          {name}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  )}
                </span>
              </label>
            </fieldset>
            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">
              <button
                className="rounded-xl border border-vitta-border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-vitta-text"
                onClick={() => setDialogOpen(false)}
                type="button"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                className="rounded-xl bg-vitta px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-60"
                disabled={pending}
                form="working-hours-form"
                type="submit"
              >
                {pending ? "Salvando..." : "Aplicar regras"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
